### [1] [FVeinSyn: Synthetic Finger Vein Image Generator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27527)
*Yifan Wang,Jie Gui,Adams Wai Kin Kong,Baosheng Yu,Changsheng Chen,Qi Li,Zhenan Sun,James Tin-Yau Kwok,Alex Kot*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出FVeinSyn，一个大规模可控的指静脉合成数据生成框架，通过解耦血管拓扑合成与成像外观渲染，生成了包含10,000个身份、共500,000张图像的合成指静脉数据集，在八个公开数据集上平均准确率提升27.43%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 指静脉识别面临大规模公开数据集缺乏的问题，现有数据集身份数量少且每指样本有限，严重制约了基于深度学习方法的发展。作者希望通过合成数据生成来解决训练样本不足导致的身份多样性不足和真实感受限等问题。

Method: FVeinSyn框架包含三个核心模块：(1)指静脉身份生成器，基于随机L系统在生理和几何约束下建模血管拓扑，生成解剖学有效且身份区分明显的血管图案；(2)级联区域感知GAN，将拓扑图渲染为逼真的近红外图像；(3)类内多样性生成器，引入几何和光学扰动以模拟真实的类内变化。

Result: 生成了500,000张图像（10,000个静脉身份，每个身份50个样本），实验表明FVeinSyn在真实感、身份多样性、血管模式一致性和类内多样性方面具有显著优势。使用FVeinSyn训练的模型在八个公开数据集上优于仅使用真实数据的基线，平均准确率提升27.43%。

Conclusion: FVeinSyn成功解决了指静脉领域合成数据生成中的身份多样性和真实感问题，为指静脉识别深度学习模型的训练提供了大规模高质量的合成数据支持，代码已开源。

Abstract: A major challenge in finger vein recognition is the lack of large-scale public datasets. Existing datasets contain few identities and limited samples per finger, restricting the advancement of deep learning-based methods. To address this, we propose FVeinSyn, a large-scale controllable synthetic data generation framework for finger vein. It explicitly decouples synthesis of vascular topology and imaging appearance to mitigate the limitations caused by insufficient training samples, such as inadequate identity diversity and restricted realism. Specifically: first, a finger vein identity generator models vascular topology under physiological and geometric constraints using stochastic L-systems, producing anatomically valid and identity-distinctive vascular patterns. Then, a cascaded region-aware GAN renders the topological maps into realistic near-infrared images. Finally, an intra-class diversity generator introduces geometric and optical perturbations to simulate realistic intra-class variations. Using FVeinSyn, we generated 500,000 images (10,000 vein identities, 50 samples per identity) and conducted extensive evaluations. Results show that FVeinSyn holds significant advantages in realism, identity diversity, vascular pattern consistency, and intra-class diversity. Models trained with FVeinSyn outperform real-data-only baselines a cross eight public datasets, achieving an average accuracy improvement of 27.43\%. The code is available at: https://github.com/EvanWang98/Synthetic-Finger-Vein-Generator.

</details>


### [2] [Revisiting Local Context for Long-Horizon Streaming 3D Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27529)
*Jiarong Han,Jincheng Xiong,Yuzhou Liu,Linzhe Shi,Changjie Wu,Ning Guo,Mu Xu,Hang Zhang,Ming Qian*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了ABot-Recon，一个仅缓存前11帧KV特征的流式3D重建模型，通过局部时间状态配合等变预测与序贯组合，实现对超长视频的稳定、有界成本的相机运动与场景几何估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有流式重建模型要么依赖有限上下文缓冲区，要么使用紧凑循环状态，在序列变长时估计精度会退化。近期方法通过耦合短程上下文与持久或层级长程记忆来改善长期稳定性，但本文选择了不同的路线——保持学习到的时间状态严格局部，同时设计与序列长度无关的预测目标。

Method: ABot-Recon仅缓存前11帧的KV特征，预测当前相机坐标系下的点图和相邻帧的相对位姿。这些预测在参考系变换下具有等变性，全局位姿和几何通过序贯组合恢复。为减少累积漂移，引入轻量级时间精化器（利用近期视觉与运动上下文改进相对旋转），并通过组合感知位姿损失监督多步位姿组合。

Result: 在Oxford Spires基准上，ABot-Recon的ATE为4.35米，RPE-R为0.12度，相比最佳先前结果将两项误差均降低了约40%。在多个挑战性长序列基准上的广泛评估证明了该局部上下文方法的优越长期性能。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，精心设计的局部上下文流式模型配合等变预测目标与组合感知损失，能够在超长视频的3D重建任务中超越依赖长程记忆机制的复杂方法，兼顾有界计算成本与高精度。

Abstract: Streaming 3D reconstruction from extremely long videos requires estimating camera motion and scene geometry online under bounded memory and computation. Early streaming models achieve causal, bounded-cost inference using finite context buffers or compact recurrent states, yet their estimates often deteriorate as sequences grow. Recent methods improve long-horizon stability by coupling short-range context with persistent or multi-level long-range memory. We pursue a different route: we keep the learned temporal state strictly local and formulate predictions whose targets remain independent of sequence length. We present ABot-Recon, a simple streaming model that caches KV features from only the preceding 11 frames. It predicts a point map in the current camera coordinate system together with an adjacent-frame relative pose. These predictions remain equivariant under changes of reference frame, and global poses and geometry are recovered through sequential composition. To reduce accumulated drift, a lightweight temporal refiner improves relative rotations using recent visual and motion context, while a composition-aware pose loss supervises multi-step pose composition. Extensive evaluations on challenging long-sequence benchmarks demonstrate the superior long-horizon performance of our local-context approach. On Oxford Spires, ABot-Recon achieves an ATE of 4.35 m and an RPE-R of $0.12^\circ$, reducing both errors by approximately 40\% relative to the best prior results.

</details>


### [3] [Code as Worlds: Agentic Discovery of Executable World Representations for Physical Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27549)
*Hanyang Wang,Yimo Cai,Weiliang Chen,Jiawei Chi,Haowen Sun,Qiyu Dai,Yi-Hsin Hung,Xingzhuo Guo,Jinshan Ren,Runmao Yao,Ziwei Liu,Mingsheng Long,Yueqi Duan,Jun Gao,Jiangran Lyu,Fangfu Liu,Jialong Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Code-as-World范式，通过可执行代码表示物理世界，并利用agentic发现循环从多模态观测中构建和验证这些表示，最终为视觉语言模型提供可扩展的物理监督信号。Code-as-World-VL在QuantiPhy基准上达到SOTA并超越领先的专有模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代视觉语言模型能识别和解释物理事件，但缺乏对底层机制（如物体状态、物理参数和动力学）的显式表示，难以可靠地推理世界如何演化及对干预做出响应。作者希望通过可执行的世界表示来弥补这一不足。

Method: 1) 引入Code-as-World范式：将物理组成、动态演化和视觉外观表示为可执行代码，形成紧凑、量化可计算、可控的世界抽象。2) 开发基于溯因推理的agentic发现循环：代理通过提议、执行、渲染、验证和迭代细化可执行世界假设，从自然语言或真实视频等多模态观测中构建世界表示。3) 使用经过验证的可执行世界为视觉语言模型提供物理监督，训练其进行定量物理推理。

Result: Code-as-World-VL在QuantiPhy基准上达到最先进性能，并超越了领先的专有模型，验证了可执行世界表示作为物理智能可扩展基础的潜力。

Conclusion: 可执行世界表示是一种有前景的范式，能够为视觉语言模型提供紧凑、可验证的物理推理基础，有望成为物理智能的可扩展基石。

Abstract: Physical understanding and reasoning depend on forming compact and generalizable representations of the world. While modern vision-language models can recognize and explain diverse physical events, they often lack explicit representations of the underlying mechanisms-such as object states, physical parameters, and governing dynamics-needed for reliably reasoning how the world evolves and responds to interventions. In this work, we introduce Code-as-World, a paradigm that represents physical worlds through executable world representations. By expressing physical composition, dynamic evolution, and visual appearance as executable code, Code-as-World provides a compact, quantitatively grounded, and controllable abstraction of the physical world. To construct such representations from multimodal observations, such as natural-language descriptions or real-world videos, we develop an agentic discovery loop inspired by abductive reasoning, where an agent proposes, executes, renders, verifies, and iteratively refines executable world hypotheses. As a concrete application, we use verified executable worlds to provide scalable physical supervision for training vision-language models on quantitative physical reasoning. Experiments show that Code-as-World-VL achieves state-of-the-art performance on QuantiPhy and surpasses leading proprietary models, highlighting the potential of executable world representations as a scalable foundation for physical intelligence.

</details>


### [4] [VidParse: Online Parsing of Egocentric Procedures Like a Pro](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27562)
*Anubhav Gupta,Archit Kambhamettu,Vatsal Agarwal,Pulkit Kumar,Abhinav Shrivastava*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VidParse是一个在线、无需训练的框架，通过将活动理解建模为图约束推理问题，利用时间相似性矩阵和过程任务图谱来解析连续嘈杂的自我中心视频流为离散的动作步骤，无需梯度更新即可实现高达10倍的复杂多步解析精度提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续、嘈杂的自我中心视频流转化为离散动作步骤面临重大视觉挑战：重度自我运动、瞬态遮挡以及无脚本人机交互的高类内变异性，导致标准帧级在线时间模型出现严重的过度分割和结构崩溃问题。

Method: 提出VidParse框架：(1)利用冻结基础模型提取操作锚定特征，优先关注前景手-物体交互；(2)通过时间相似性矩阵动态识别语义转换；(3)使用束搜索解码器，结合诱导出的过程任务图谱来强制有效的动作转换并剪除不可能的轨迹。

Result: 在复杂多步解析精度上比强在线基线方法提升高达10倍，且不需要任何梯度更新，同时保持长期状态转换。

Conclusion: 通过将稳健的视觉片段锚定到硬性过程约束上，有效弥合了不稳定的低层感知与高层过程逻辑之间的差距，为在线活动理解提供了一种训练无关的替代方案。

Abstract: Translating continuous, noisy egocentric video streams into discrete, temporally ordered action steps is fraught with visual challenges. Heavy ego-motion, transient occlusions, and the high intra-class variability of unscripted human-object interactions cause standard frame-level online temporal models to struggle, often resulting in severe over-segmentation and structural collapse. To bridge the gap between unstable low-level perception and high-level procedural logic, we present VidParse, an online, training-free framework that treats activity understanding as a graph-constrained inference problem. Rather than relying on learned temporal filters, we dynamically identify semantic transitions using a temporal similarity matrix over manipulation-anchored features, which are extracted from frozen foundation models to prioritize foreground hand-object interactions. A beam search decoder then leverages an induced procedural task graph to explicitly enforce valid action transitions and prune impossible trajectories. By anchoring robust visual segments to hard procedural constraints, our approach preserves long-range state transitions and achieves up to a 10x improvement in complex multi-step parsing accuracy over strong online baselines, all without requiring a single gradient update.

</details>


### [5] [Quanta Perception as Probabilistic Events](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27584)
*Varun Sundar,Pavan Thodima,Sacha Jungerman,Mohit Gupta*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出"概率事件"（probabilistic events）这一计算原语，通过递归贝叶斯推断直接从单光子检测流中实时感知场景，替代传统的帧重建方法，在极低光照和高速动态场景下实现千赫兹级输出，比现有最先进的quanta重建方法快四个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统传感器在极端环境（如夜间导航、高速机器人）下存在灵敏度、动态范围和时域分辨率的固有权衡；而量子（quanta）传感器虽能检测单光子，但其数据流超出实时计算和延迟预算数个数量级，现有方法无法实现实时感知。

Method: 提出概率事件计算原语：通过计算自上次强度变化以来的时间后验分布，将光子流表示为递归信念状态。基于递归贝叶斯框架，产生三种低延迟信号：运动自适应场景通量、高保真活动图、基于熵的感知不确定性。直接在光子流上进行概率推断，而非重建帧。

Result: 在约0.05 lux极低光照下实现跑步人体姿态估计（无需重新训练视觉模型）；在商品GPU硬件上处理超过50,000量子帧/秒的输入流，产生千赫兹级输出，比最先进的量子重建基线快达四个数量级，且支持百万像素级阵列。

Conclusion: 通过用直接概率推断替代帧重建，该工作将光子计数型量子传感与机器人视觉连接起来，为极端环境下的自主系统感知提供了新范式。

Abstract: Autonomous systems rely on extracting information from light, yet remain brittle in extreme environments, from nighttime navigation to high-speed robotics. Conventional sensors aggregate photons over fixed exposures, imposing trade-offs between sensitivity, dynamic range, and temporal resolution that degrade perception when photons are scarce or dynamics are rapid. Quanta sensors detect individual photons, but their streams exceed real-time compute and latency budgets by orders of magnitude.
  Here we introduce $\textit{probabilistic events}$, a computational primitive for real-time quanta perception from individual photon detections. By computing the posterior over the time since the last intensity change, we represent photon streams as recursive belief states. Rather than fixed-threshold event-camera triggers, this recursive Bayesian formulation yields three low-latency signals: motion-adaptive scene flux, high-fidelity activity maps, and entropy-based perceptual uncertainty. This representation enables perception in extreme conditions, including pose estimation of a running person at $\sim$0.05 lux---without retraining vision models. Our approach processes input streams exceeding 50{,}000 quanta frames per second on commodity GPU hardware---yielding kilohertz-scale outputs up to four orders of magnitude faster than state-of-the-art quanta reconstruction baselines, even for megapixel arrays. By replacing frame reconstruction with direct probabilistic inference over photon streams, this work bridges photon-counting quanta sensing with robotic vision.

</details>


### [6] [ShiftSplit-AD: Separating Domain Shift from Defects in Foundation-Feature Visual Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27610)
*Muhamathu Ameer Ali Aacaas Muhamath*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出ShiftSplit-AD方法，通过将DINOv2特征残差矩阵分解为低秩和行稀疏分量，仅对稀疏分量评分以抑制域偏移干扰，同时保留未知缺陷信号。实验表明该方法在强域偏移场景（AeBAD-S）下能显著提升异常检测性能，但也暴露出一个核心权衡：真实缺陷可能包含低秩结构，稀疏化处理会同时丢失缺陷信息，导致干净样本误报率上升和定位性能下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于冻结基础模型特征的视觉异常检测器通常通过计算测试补丁到正常特征记忆库的距离来评分，但良性采集变化（域偏移）也会扩大这些距离，导致域变异与缺陷互相混淆。作者希望探究是否可以通过结构化解构最近正常残差来抑制域偏移带来的干扰，同时保留未知缺陷的真实信号。

Method: ShiftSplit-AD将每个测试补丁的残差矩阵（与最近正常特征的差异）分解为低秩分量和行稀疏分量两个部分，仅对行稀疏分量进行异常评分，并可选地采用低秩/稀疏融合策略。这种方法旨在将域偏移相关的宽泛残差活动归入低秩结构，而将缺陷相关的稀疏信号分离出来。

Result: 在AeBAD-S数据集上，仅使用稀疏评分的方案将图像AUROC从0.6780提升至0.7294，AUPRC从0.8052提升至0.8465，配对bootstrap 95%置信区间分别为[0.0238, 0.0808]和[0.0170, 0.0650]，改进显著。但在四个保留的MVTec类别上，平均干净AUROC从0.9890下降至0.9133，且Bottle类别定位性能退化。

Conclusion: 残差分解在域偏移强烈污染异常证据的场景下是有帮助的，但真实缺陷可能包含相关的低维结构，过滤宽泛残差活动也会移除部分缺陷信息。因此，残差分解可以部分缓解域偏移问题，但保留缺陷结构仍是核心挑战和限制因素。

Abstract: Visual anomaly detectors based on frozen foundation-model features commonly score distances from test patches to a memory of normal features. Benign acquisition changes can also enlarge these distances, confounding domain variation with defects. We investigate whether structured decomposition of nearest-normal DINOv2 residuals can suppress shift-induced evidence while retaining unseen defects. ShiftSplit-AD decomposes the patch residual matrix into low-rank and row-sparse components and scores the sparse component, with an optional low-rank/sparse fusion. The experiments expose a central trade-off rather than a universal separation: genuine defects can contain correlated, low-dimensional structure, so filtering broad residual activity may also remove defect information. On AeBAD-S, using settings fixed after Bottle development, sparse-only scoring improves image AUROC from 0.6780 to 0.7294 and AUPRC from 0.8052 to 0.8465. Paired bootstrap 95% intervals for the improvements are [0.0238, 0.0808] and [0.0170, 0.0650], respectively. However, sparse-only scoring reduces mean clean AUROC from 0.9890 to 0.9133 on four held-out MVTec categories and degrades Bottle localization. These findings show that residual decomposition can help when domain shift strongly contaminates anomaly evidence, but preserving defect structure remains the limiting problem.

</details>


### [7] [Depth-Aware Pothole Detection Using YOLO and RT-DETR at the Edge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27633)
*Md Monjurul Ahsan Prodhan,Md Nour Hossain*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种深度感知的坑洼检测框架，比较了五种架构（YOLOv8n、YOLOv8nSeg、YOLOv9t、RTDETRL、RTDETRX）在RGB-D传感器融合下的检测性能和深度测量精度。实验表明YOLOv8nSeg在精度和深度估计上表现最佳，且发现边界框模型存在结构性路面包含偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动化坑洼检测依赖2D RGB图像，无法测量坑洼的物理深度，导致维护滞后并引发车辆损坏和交通事故。需要一种能够同时检测坑洼并测量其真实深度的深度感知框架。

Method: 使用RGB-D传感器融合，构建深度感知坑洼检测框架，比较YOLOv8n、YOLOv8nSeg、YOLOv9t、RTDETRL和RTDETRX五种架构。采用自定义离线增强管道模拟恶劣道路监测条件，使用RANSAC地面平面正交校正修正深度图相机倾斜，并将零值传感器像素转为NaN。训练数据集为PothRGBD（80%训练/20%验证）。

Result: YOLOv8nSeg取得最高mAP@50（0.9556）和mAP@50_95（0.6758），深度估计最准确（2.96cm，采用Dseg算法）。YOLOv8n推理速度最快（3.6ms），RTDETRX检测置信度最高（92.70%）。即便经过完全RANSAC校正，边界框模型仍比像素级分割掩码高估坑洼深度0.16-0.21cm。

Conclusion: 像素级分割模型（YOLOv8nSeg）在坑洼检测精度和深度测量准确性上均优于边界框模型。路面包含偏差是结构性问题而非校正伪影，说明采用分割掩码进行深度测量更为可靠。

Abstract: Pothole detection and its severity measurement is still an important challenges in urban infrastructure management, where late maintenance directly contributes to vehicle damage, road accidents, and escalating repair costs. Existing automated approaches depend on 2D RGB images and cannot measure physical depth of potholes. In this paper, we present a depthaware pothole detection framework and then compare five architectures: YOLOv8n, YOLOv8nSeg, YOLOv9t, RTDETRL, and RTDETRX for RGB-D sensor fusion-based detection and automated depth measurement. A custom offline augmentation pipeline is used here to simulate adverse road monitoring conditions. All models are trained on the PothRGBD dataset with an 80% training and 20% validation split and evaluated using Precision, Recall, mAP@50, and mAP@50_95. Before measuring the depth data, all depth maps are corrected for camera tilt using RANSAC ground-plane orthorectification and all zero-valued sensor pixels are cast to NaN before any statistic is computed. YOLOv8nSeg achieves the highest mAP@50 of 0.9556 and mAP@50_95 of 0.6758 with the most accurate depth estimate of 2.96 cm with the pixel-precise Dseg algorithm. YOLOv8n achieves the fastest inference at 3.6ms. RTDETRX achieves the highest detection confidence at 92.70%. An important finding is that even after full RANSAC orthorectification, bounding box models overestimate pothole depth by 0.16 to 0.21 cm compared to pixel precise segmentation masks. This confirms that the pavement inclusion bias is structural rather than a calibration artifact.

</details>


### [8] [Report Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27668)
*Pedro R. A. S. Bassia,Wenxuan Li,Jakob Wasserthal,Jieneng Chen,Xinze Zhou,Zheren Zhu,Chuntung Zhuanga,Sergio Decherchi,Andrea Cavalli,Kang Wang,Yang Yang,Alan Yuille,Zongwei Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出R-Super训练框架，利用放射学报告（而非仅依赖肿瘤掩膜）直接监督和提升肿瘤分割模型的性能。通过新的损失函数，让模型学习分割与报告中描述的肿瘤数量、大小和位置一致的区域。在肾脏和胰腺肿瘤分割任务上，R-Super在外部验证中相比仅用掩膜训练将F1分数和DSC提升最多15%，并在掩膜数据稀缺或充足时均显著优于CLIP和多任务学习等替代方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 肿瘤分割模型的瓶颈在于肿瘤掩膜稀缺——制作一个3D掩膜需时长达30分钟，公共数据集仅含几百个掩膜，最大私有数据集也只有数千个。然而，放射学报告在临床常规中大量产生（公共数据集含数万对CT-报告，医院含数十万对），且报告详细描述了肿瘤特征。现有研究未能利用这些大规模易获取的报告来直接提升分割性能，因此亟需一种利用报告监督训练分割模型的新框架。

Method: 本文提出Report Supervision (R-Super)训练框架，核心思路是利用放射学报告直接监督肿瘤分割模型的训练。框架引入了新的损失函数，教导分割模型生成与报告中描述的肿瘤数量、大小和位置相匹配的分割结果。报告仅用于训练阶段，推理时无需额外开销。实验涵盖了不同规模的训练数据（最多41,418对CT-报告和3,488对胰腺肿瘤CT-掩膜），并在肾脏和胰腺肿瘤分割任务上进行评估。

Result: 在外部验证集上，R-Super相比仅用掩膜训练将肿瘤检测F1分数和分割DSC提升最多15%。该方法也超越了CLIP和多任务学习等替代方法。更具体地，R-Super在训练掩膜非常稀缺（如仅50个）和掩膜充足（如3,488个）两种场景下均显著提升AI性能，验证了利用大量现成报告补充稀缺掩膜的有效性，解锁了肿瘤分割的规模化训练能力。

Conclusion: R-Super证明放射学报告可以作为一种大规模、低成本的有效监督信号，显著增强肿瘤分割性能。该框架不仅弥补了掩膜稀缺的短板，还能在掩膜数量充足时进一步提升性能，为肿瘤分割模型的规模化训练提供了新的路径，具有重要的临床转化潜力。

Abstract: Segmentation models can surpass radiologists, classification models, and vision-language models in tumor detection. Importantly, segmentation models outline tumors, allowing radiologists to better verify and trust the AI output. Their main limitation is the scarcity of tumor masks: creating one 3D tumor mask takes up to 30 minutes, so most public CT datasets contain only a few hundred masks, and even the largest private datasets contain only a couple of thousand. Tumor masks are not produced in clinical routine, but radiology reports are. Public datasets contain tens of thousands of CT-Report pairs, and hospitals contain hundreds of thousands. These reports describe tumors in detail, providing large-scale, informative training data. Here, we introduce Report Supervision (R-Super), a training framework that uses reports to directly supervise and improve tumor segmentation. R-Super introduces new loss functions that teach segmentation models to segment tumors that match report descriptions of tumor count, sizes, and locations. Reports are only used for training. We evaluated R-Super on kidney and pancreatic tumor segmentation, exploring diverse training data sizes, up to 41,418 CT-Report plus 3,488 pancreatic tumor CT-Mask pairs. On external validation, R-Super increased tumor detection F1-Score and segmentation DSC by up to +15% with respect to mask-only training. It also surpassed alternative methods such as CLIP and multi-task learning. Leveraging numerous readily available reports to supplement scarce masks, R-Super strongly improves AI performance when very few training masks are available (e.g., 50), and when many masks are available (e.g., 3,488), unlocking scale in tumor segmentation.

</details>


### [9] [ABCD: Alpha-Composited Block Coordinate Descent: Constant-VRAM Training for Large Radiance Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27735)
*Ka Heng Shiu,Kartic Subr*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出ABCD框架，一种用于alpha混合辐射场（以3D高斯泼溅为例）的越核（out-of-core）训练方法。通过将训练重构为块坐标下降，每次只激活一个空间块参数，利用alpha混合的结合性将非活动区域预渲染为前景和背景RGBA图像，使得峰值显存对场景规模达到O(1)复杂度，从而让有限显存的GPU也能训练超大场景，且重构质量损失极小（PSNR下降<5%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3D高斯泼溅等辐射场方法在训练时显存需求随场景规模线性增长，限制了显存有限的GPU处理大规模场景的能力。现有方法难以在有限显存下训练超出核心内存容量的场景。

Method: 将训练过程重构为块坐标下降（Block Coordinate Descent），对空间进行分区，每次仅激活一个参数块而冻结其他块。利用alpha混合的结合性，将非活动区域的预渲染结果折叠为前景和背景RGBA图像，从而避免全场景在显存中驻留。当分区大小和图像分辨率固定时，峰值显存对场景总规模为O(1)。

Result: 实验表明ABCD紧密保持了3DGS的重构质量，PSNR退化小于5%；而消融了合成（compositing）机制的版本则有约40%的质量退化，证明了核心合成模块的有效性。

Conclusion: ABCD使有限显存GPU能够训练原本无法装入核心内存的大规模场景，同时保持高质量重构，为越核辐射场训练提供了一种有效方案，代码已开源。

Abstract: We present ABCD (Alpha-Composited Block Coordinate Descent), an out-of-core training framework for alpha-composited radiance fields, instantiated here for 3D Gaussian Splatting. Our method reformulates training as block coordinate descent over spatial partitions: only one block of parameters is active at a time, while all others are frozen. By exploiting the associativity of alpha blending, these inactive regions can be pre-rendered and collapsed into foreground and background RGBA images.
  As a result, for fixed partition size and image resolution, peak VRAM becomes O(1) with respect to total scene extent, rather than growing with full scene size. This enables GPUs with limited memory to train scenes that would otherwise not fit in core. In experiments, our method closely preserves the reconstruction quality of 3DGS, with less than 5% PSNR degradation, while ABCD with compositing ablated suffers roughly 40% degradation.
  Our code can be found at https://github.com/shiukaheng/abcd

</details>


### [10] [What Can Low Resource Languages Learn From Each Other?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27753)
*Achyuth P,Kahaan Shah,Chetan Arora*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出PSMC框架，通过"预训练-特化-合并-联合训练"四步策略，利用跨脚本"迁移效应"在极端数据稀缺条件下实现多语言OCR适配，在10种印度文字（支持20+语言）上较个体专家模型平均提升约2%的单词识别率，且不增加参数量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型（VLM）的语言覆盖范围主要局限于高资源语言，低资源文字在OCR任务中因缺乏大规模数据集而难以通过传统扩展法则获得性能提升。现有的语言专用微调策略存在结构性低效：模型高层特征虽然分化以捕捉独特文字特性，但低层特征冗余且高度相似，造成计算资源的浪费。

Method: 提出PSMC四阶段框架：(1) Pre-train：从高资源基础模型出发；(2) Specialize：针对各低资源语言派生专门的专家模型；(3) Merge：利用任务算术将多个专家模型融合为统一的多语言骨干模型；(4) Co-train：在合并后的潜在空间中进行联合训练，以促进跨脚本的建设性知识迁移。该方法在极端数据稀缺（<10K真实图像和<250K合成图像）条件下运作。

Result: 在10种印度文字（支持20+语言）上的广泛评估显示，PSMC相比个体专家模型在单词识别率（WRR）上平均提升约2%，且不增加参数数量。结果表明在合并的潜在空间中进行联合训练能够实现有利于所有组成文字的建设性知识迁移。

Conclusion: PSMC提供了一种在数据极端稀缺条件下可扩展的低资源语言OCR适配路径，证明跨脚本知识迁移是缩小数字鸿沟的有效策略，为包容性VLM开发提供了新思路。代码和数据集将在论文发表后公开。

Abstract: Despite the rapid advancement of Vision-Language Models (VLMs), their linguistic reach remains largely confined to high-resource languages, leaving the majority of the world's 7,000+ living languages on the wrong side of a growing digital divide. This disparity is especially pronounced in Optical Character Recognition (OCR), where low-resource scripts lack the massive datasets required for traditional scaling laws. We investigate OCR adaptation in extreme data-scarce regimes (<10K real and <250K synthetic images), demonstrating that conventional fine-tuning strategies often reach a performance ceiling. Our key finding reveals a structural inefficiency in language-specific adaptation: while higher layers of specialized models diverge to capture unique script nuances, the lower layers learn redundant, highly similar features. Motivated by this observation, we propose PSMC (Pre-train, Specialize, Merge, and Co-train), a data-efficient framework that capitalizes on a cross-script "transfer effect". Our approach first derives language-specific experts from a high-resource base model, then employs task arithmetic to fuse these experts into a unified, high-performance multilingual back- bone. Extensive evaluation across 10 Indian scripts (supporting 20+ languages) shows that PSMC achieves a ~2% average improvement in Word Recognition Rate (WRR) over individual specialist models without increasing parameter count. Our results indicate that joint training in the merged latent space facilitates a constructive knowledge transfer that benefits all constituent scripts, providing a scalable pathway for inclusive VLM development. Source code and datasets will be released post publication.

</details>


### [11] [uScenes: A Multimodal RGB and 3D Sonar Dataset for Underwater Robot Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27795)
*Trung Tien Dong,Zhenqi Wu,Aditya Penumarti,Zi-Hao Zhang,Micaiah Bartlett,Jane Shin,Xiaomin Lin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了uScenes，一个包含同步3D多波束声纳点云和RGB图像的多模态水下数据集，包含110个场景和95,834个同步观测，解决了水下光学相机在低光照和反向散射条件下失效、以及2D声纳缺乏仰角信息导致的3D定位模糊问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水下自主机器人的可靠感知至关重要，但光学相机在低光照和反向散射条件下不可靠；前视2D声纳虽在恶劣条件下有效，但只能测量距离和方位角，无法解析仰角，导致声纳回波无法在3D空间中定位，阻碍了3D场景理解和精确目标检测。

Method: 构建了uScenes多模态水下数据集，通过同步采集3D多波束声纳点云和RGB图像，涵盖110个场景和95,834个同步观测，总计277.6分钟数据，采集自多个现场会话。

Result: 数据集包含110个场景、95,834个同步观测，覆盖277.6分钟的现场采集数据，为水下传感器融合、跨模态表示学习和3D场景理解提供了基础资源，代码和数据集已开源。

Conclusion: uScenes为水下感知领域建立了一个多模态数据集基础，可支持水下传感器融合、跨模态表示学习和3D场景理解等研究方向的发展。

Abstract: Robust perception is essential for the deployment of autonomous underwater robots. However, optical cameras become unreliable under poor illumination and backscatter. Forward looking (2D) acoustic sensors remain effective under these conditions, but they measure range and bearing while leaving elevation unresolved, creating an ambiguity that prevents individual sonar returns from being localized in three dimensional (3D) space. This complicates the sensor use for 3D scene understanding and precise object detection. We introduce \textbf{uScenes}, a multimodal underwater dataset containing synchronized 3D multibeam sonar point clouds and RGB imagery. The dataset contains 110 scenes and 95,834 synchronized observation, representing 277.6 minutes of data collected across multiple field sessions. uScenes establishes a foundation for underwater sensor fusion, cross modal representation learning and 3D scene understanding. Code and datasets are given at https://github.com/era-research-lab/uScenes.

</details>


### [12] [From Perspective to Fisheye Depth Estimation and Open-Vocabulary Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27860)
*Rit Gangopadhyay,Alex Wong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种名为DEX（Distortion Extenders）的方法，通过引入一组可学习的参数来建模鱼眼图像的畸变系数及潜在空间中的分布偏移，从而使视觉基础模型能够泛化到鱼眼相机拍摄的宽视场图像上，在深度估计和开放词汇分割等任务中均优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉基础模型在透视图像上表现优异，但当应用于鱼眼相机拍摄的宽视场图像时，由于径向畸变导致像素层面的协变量偏移，模型输出错误结果。现有方法缺乏对鱼眼图像的泛化能力，因此需要一种通用的解决方案。

Method: 提出一组可学习的参数DEX，用于建模鱼眼畸变系数以及鱼眼图像与透视图像在潜在空间中的分布偏移。通过最小化自监督对齐损失，将鱼眼图像的潜在嵌入变换为接近透视图像的表示，从而恢复高保真估计。DEX与网络架构和任务无关，可适配卷积和Transformer架构。

Result: 在单目深度估计和开放词汇分割任务上，针对卷积和Transformer架构，室内外鱼眼数据集上均一致优于基线方法。此外，DEX的激活值还可解码为畸变系数，辅助相机标定。

Conclusion: DEX为视觉基础模型向鱼眼相机泛化提供了一种架构无关、任务无关的有效方案，并额外支持相机标定功能，具有广泛的应用前景。

Abstract: Vision foundation models are capable of generalizing across 3-dimensional (3D) scenes with high-fidelity estimates; their empirical success can be attributed to training on large-scale datasets of perspective images. However, when transferred to wide field-of-view (FoV) images, such as those captured by fisheye cameras, they return erroneous outputs due to a covariate shift stemming from the radial distortion on the image pixels. We propose a method to generalize vision foundation models to fisheye cameras. The crux of our method lies in a set of learnable parameters, termed Distortion Extenders (DEX), that model the fisheye distortion coefficients and the distributional shift between fisheye and perspective images encoded in the latent space. By minimizing a self-supervised alignment loss, DEX transforms the latent embeddings of fisheye images to resemble those of perspective images to recover high-fidelity estimates. DEX is architecture- and task-agnostic: We demonstrate DEX on monocular depth estimation and open-vocabulary segmentation for convolution- and Transformer-based architectures, where we consistently improve over baselines across indoor and outdoor fisheye datasets. As a byproduct, the activations of DEX can also be decoded to distortion coefficients to support camera calibration. Code available at: https://github.com/Suchisrit/DEX.

</details>


### [13] [Iron: Intent-Aligned and Retrospective Dual Learning Framework for Enhancing Generalist Virtual Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27866)
*Jiahe Ying,Wendong Bu,Kaihang Pan,Bingchen Miao,Siyu Chen,Wen Wang,Xueming Jiang,Juncheng Li,Siliang Tang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Iron框架，一个面向GUI智能体的意图对齐、自我改进且标注高效的训练框架。Iron通过步进循环一致性（SCC）奖励实现低层动作与高层意图的细粒度对齐，并引入事后重放机制将失败轨迹重新用于训练，显著提升跨环境和跨设备任务的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虚拟智能体在多样化数字环境中自动化任务仍是具身AI领域的关键挑战。多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）虽增强了视觉感知和推理能力，但其代理部署面临三大挑战：数据标注成本高、动作-意图对齐不精确、失败轨迹被丢弃导致探索效率低下。

Method: Iron框架采用双重学习策略：1）提出步进循环一致性（SCC）奖励机制，实现低层动作与高层意图之间的细粒度对齐，改善指令理解和意图推理；2）引入事后重放（hindsight reproduction）机制，将失败轨迹重新利用于训练，提升学习效率和任务多样性。

Result: 实验表明，Iron训练的通用智能体在跨环境和跨设备任务上持续提升性能，优于使用三倍数据训练的模型；在未见过的网页任务上实现了25.06%的相对提升，在复杂任务上还有进一步增益。

Conclusion: Iron证明了在数据效率和意图对齐方面的创新能够构建更强大的虚拟智能体，为GUI智能体训练提供了可行的高效路径。

Abstract: Achieving virtual agents capable of automating tasks across diverse digital environments remains a pivotal challenge in Embodied AI. While Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) offer enhanced visual perception and reasoning, their agentic deployment faces three challenges: costly data annotation, imprecise action-intent alignment, and inefficient exploration from discarded failed trajectories. To address these, we introduce Iron, an intent-aligned, self-improved, and annotation-efficient framework for training GUI agents. Iron employs a novel dual learning strategy that utilizes a stepwise cycle-consistent (SCC) reward to achieve fine-grained alignment between low-level actions and high-level intents, thereby improving instruction grounding and intent understanding. Concurrently, Iron introduces a hindsight reproduction mechanism to repurpose failed trajectories for training, improving both learning efficiency and task diversity. Extensive experiments demonstrate that Iron-trained generalist agents consistently improve performance on cross-environment and cross-device tasks, outperforming models trained with three times more data. Iron also achieves a substantial 25.06% relative improvement on unseen web tasks, with further gains observed on inherently complex tasks, demonstrating the feasibility of building more capable virtual agents.

</details>


### [14] [Temporal Tree of Thought: Reasoning-Guided Visual Cue Search for Long-Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27871)
*Ziling Huang,Shin'ichi Satoh*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出T³（Temporal Tree of Thought），一种无需训练的粗到细自适应长视频理解框架。通过构建基于递归时间约束聚类的层次化时间树，并在推理时执行"回答-检索-探索"循环，自适应地从时间区域定位到具体对象和视觉细节，在三个基准数据集上均取得提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长视频理解面临上下文长度限制，均匀采样会遗漏关键时刻，基于智能体的方法往往独立评估帧而忽略视频的时间组织。现有方法缺乏类似人类"先定位相关片段、再放大细节"的层次化证据选择机制。

Method: T³框架包含两个核心组件：1）构建问题无关的层次化时间树，通过递归时间约束聚类生成连续片段节点，每个节点含信息量丰富的关键帧；2）推理时执行"回答-检索-探索"循环，先在粗粒度代表帧上推理，证据不足时生成搜索语句并扩展相关分支以获取更细粒度证据。

Result: 在VideoMME、LongVideoBench和LVBench上，T³在相同帧预算下分别将Qwen2.5-VL-7B提升0.5%、4.6%和4.4%，验证了结构化时间推理的有效性。

Conclusion: T³通过自适应地从粗到细的时间推理范式，模拟人类观看长视频时的分段定位与细节聚焦过程，有效提升了长视频理解的性能，且无需额外训练。

Abstract: Long-video understanding remains challenging for Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) due to limited context length. Uniform sampling may miss crucial moments, while agent-based frame video understanding methods often evaluate frames independently, overlooking the temporal organization of videos. Ideally, evidence selection should mimic how humans answer questions about long videos: first locating the relevant segment from the global context, then zooming into local objects and details. We propose Temporal Tree of Thought T^3, a training-free framework for adaptive coarse-to-fine long-video understanding. T^3 constructs a question-agnostic hierarchical temporal tree via recursive temporally constrained clustering, where each node represents a contiguous segment with an informative key frame. During inference, T^3 performs an answer-retrieve-explore loop: it reasons over coarse representative frames, generates a search statement when evidence is insufficient, and expands relevant branches for finer-grained evidence. This process adaptively shifts the search target from temporal regions to specific objects and visual details to help video understanding. Experiments on VideoMME, LongVideoBench, and LVBench show that T^3 improves Qwen2.5-VL-7B by 0.5%, 4.6%, and 4.4%, respectively, under the same frame budget, demonstrating the effectiveness of structured temporal reasoning.

</details>


### [15] [Relational Knowledge Distillation Brings DNN Representations Close Enough to Humans to Be Aligned Without Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27877)
*Yuria Shimizu,Soh Takahashi,Takato Horii,Masafumi Oizumi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究了通过关系知识蒸馏（RKD）将人类表征的关系结构迁移到深度神经网络（DNN）中，并使用Gromov-Wasserstein最优传输（GWOT）进行无监督对齐评估。结果显示，经过RKD微调后的预训练DNN能够在与训练数据不重叠的测试集上实现个体对象级别的细粒度对齐，且这种改进主要源于全局结构的类人化，而非局部最近邻重叠率的提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将DNN的内部表征与人类心理表征对齐对于使用DNN作为人类视觉的计算模型至关重要。已有研究表明关系迁移方法能改善人类-DNN表征相似性，但这种改进是否能在更严格的评估标准下成立（包括个体对象水平的细粒度对齐、以及独立于训练数据的数据集上的泛化能力）此前未被验证。

Method: 使用两种方法进行严格评估：（1）采用无监督的Gromov-Wasserstein最优传输（GWOT）方法，仅从内部距离结构估计人类-DNN对应关系，测试细粒度对齐；（2）在与训练数据概念不重叠的精选测试集上评估泛化能力。同时对预训练DNN使用Relational Knowledge Distillation (RKD)进行微调，以迁移人类表征的关系结构。

Result: 研究发现：RKD微调后的预训练DNN在独立测试集上能够实现个体对象级别的细粒度人类-DNN对齐。进一步分析表明，这种改进主要由全局结构的类人化驱动（体现在粗粒度类别间距离的排序上），而局部人类-DNN最近邻重叠率基本保持不变。

Conclusion: 关系迁移方法能够将预训练DNN的全局结构调整到与人类结构足够接近，从而无需监督即可实现细粒度的人类-DNN对齐。这表明从人类迁移关系结构是弥合DNN与人类视觉表征差距的有效途径，但改进主要体现在全局结构而非局部特征上。

Abstract: Linking the internal representations of deep neural networks (DNNs) to human mental representations is important for using DNNs as computational models of human vision. Existing DNN representations remain insufficiently similar to human mental representations, which are not directly observable and are therefore commonly measured through large-scale similarity judgments of object images. A natural approach to narrowing this gap is to directly transfer the relational structure of human representations into DNNs, and previous studies have reported improved human-DNN representational similarity. However, whether this improvement holds under stricter evaluation remains untested in two respects: fine-grained alignment at the individual-object level, and generalization to a human embedding derived from a dataset independent of the training data. Here, we employ an unsupervised comparison method, Gromov-Wasserstein optimal transport (GWOT), which estimates human-DNN correspondences from the internal distance structure alone and thereby tests fine-grained alignment. We further assess generalization on a curated test set of concepts non-overlapping with the training data. We show that fine-tuning pre-trained DNNs with Relational Knowledge Distillation (RKD), an established relational transfer method, brings DNNs close enough to humans to be aligned at the individual-object level on this test set. We also show that this improvement is driven by a more human-like global structure, as reflected in the ordering of distances among coarse categories, while the local human-DNN nearest-neighbor overlap rate remains largely unchanged. These findings indicate that relational transfer from humans brings the global structure of pre-trained DNNs close enough to the human structure to enable fine-grained human-DNN alignment without supervision.

</details>


### [16] [What Do Interaction Representations Actually Measure? Pre-Event Separability in Weakly-Supervised Violence Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27879)
*Parishruthi Ganesh*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文在暴力检测任务中系统比较了多种人体姿态表征与粗粒度边界框几何特征，发现姿态表征未能超越几何特征；进一步对比裁剪人物外观与整帧上下文，发现整帧上下文表现更优。通过仅在标注事件起始前帧评分的诊断实验，揭示了视频级AUC中包含大量来源伪影（如标题卡片、平台水印）导致的预事件线索，这些线索构成了判别力的重要来源，可能掩盖不同表征间的真实差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然人体姿态提供了比粗粒度空间关系更详细的身体构型信息，但在固定下游管线的情况下，这些细节是否能带来更强的判别信息仍不清楚。作者希望通过暴力检测任务进行严格对照实验来回答这一问题。

Method: 固定跟踪器、时间头、监督方式、数据划分和评估协议，对比五种交互表征（粗粒度边界框几何、匹配的手工姿态模拟、增强姿态描述子、从原始关节点学习的匹配容量编码器），采用视频级评估与聚类自助区间。扩展至冻结视觉编码器管线，在UCF-Crime和XD-Violence两个基准上比较人物裁剪外观与整帧上下文。最后设计诊断实验：仅使用标注事件起始前的帧对异常视频打分，并通过控制序列长度线索，检验基准实际测量的内容。

Result: 无姿态表征超越粗粒度几何特征（但15个异常视频的样本量不足以排除小效应）。在XD-Violence上（137个异常视频）人物裁剪外观和整帧上下文均大幅超越几何特征，但上下文在UCF-Crime上与外观持平、在更大规模划分上超越外观，裁剪交互人物区域相对整帧编码没有优势。仅用事件起始前帧评分的对照实验中保留了39-91%的超过随机水平的判别力，预事件窗口内发现编辑标题卡片和平台水印等来源伪影。

Conclusion: 视频级AUC是事件证据与预事件来源线索的复合指标，这一共有的判别力来源可能掩盖不同表征之间的真实差异。该诊断方法仅需基准已提供的事件标注即可实施。

Abstract: Articulated human pose provides detailed body-configuration information beyond coarse spatial relationships, but whether this detail yields greater discriminative information when the downstream pipeline is held fixed remains unclear. We examine this through early violence detection. Holding the tracker, temporal head, supervision, folds, and evaluation fixed, we compare five interaction representations spanning coarse bounding-box geometry, a matched handcrafted pose analogue, enriched pose descriptors, and a matched-capacity encoder learned from raw joints, under video-level evaluation with cluster-bootstrap intervals. No pose-based representation outperforms coarse geometry, though with fifteen anomalous videos this subset cannot rule out small effects. Extending the pipeline to frozen visual encoders, and repeating the comparison on XD-Violence (137 anomalous videos, nine times our UCF-Crime sample), person-crop appearance and whole-frame context both exceed geometry by a wide margin, yet context matches appearance on UCF-Crime and exceeds it on the larger split: cropping to the interacting people yields no advantage over encoding the whole frame. This prompts a direct test of what the benchmark measures. Scoring anomalous videos using only frames preceding the annotated onset, under a control removing sequence length as a cue, retains 39-91% of above-chance separation on both benchmarks, including for seven hand-designed geometric channels. Inspection of the tightest pre-onset windows identifies concrete provenance artifacts: editorial title cards and platform watermarks absent from the surveillance footage supplying the normal class. Video-level AUC here is thus a composite of event evidence and pre-event source cues, a shared source of discrimination that can obscure differences between representations. The diagnostic requires only annotations these benchmarks already ship.

</details>


### [17] [StreamEMS: Streaming Video Understanding with Self-Evolving Memory Scheme for Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27881)
*Yuxin Liu,Peiqin Zhuang,Yali Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出StreamEMS，一种通过自进化记忆机制重新结构化存储在记忆中的历史数据来提升流式视频理解能力的方法，在OVO-Bench和StreamingBench上取得优于现有方法的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有流式视频理解方法通过构建外部记忆来存储历史数据以减少计算量，但大多数方法仅关注当前数据的写入（write）和历史数据信息的检索（read）流程优化，忽视了提升记忆本身表征能力的可能性。

Method: StreamEMS包含两个核心模块：1) 语义进化模块（Semantic Evolution Module），通过从粗到细逐步缩小的语义尺度发现信息性记忆实体，将记忆演化为更信息密集的表征；2) 先验引导进化模块（Prior-informed Evolution Module），利用先验记忆分布来精炼当前记忆状态，使记忆表征更加鲁棒。

Result: 在OVO-Bench和StreamingBench两个广泛使用的流式视频理解数据集上验证了方法的有效性，结果显示优于其他方法。即使在较高的token使用率下降设置下，方法的优势依然保持一致，证明了其在挖掘记忆本身潜力方面的有效性和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 通过重新结构化和进化记忆本身的表征能力，而非仅优化读写流程，可以有效提升流式视频理解的性能和鲁棒性，尤其是在高计算效率要求下仍能保持优势。

Abstract: Recently, many streaming video understanding methods have been proposed by constructing an external memory to store historical data for computational reduction. Most methods focus on optimizing the injection procedure of current data (write) and retrieving informative historical data (read) from memory, while overlooking the opportunity to further enhancing the representational capability of memory itself. In this work, we present StreamEMS, a general mechanism for improving streaming video understanding by re-structuring the historical data stored in memory through self-evolving memory scheme, enabling more informative and robust memory representations. Specifically, we first introduce a Semantic Evolution Module to evolve the memory into more information-dense representations by exploiting informative memory entities discovered via progressively shrinking semantic scales from coarse to fine. In addition, we further introduce a Prior-informed Evolution Module to evolve memory into more robust representations by leveraging prior memory distributions to refine the current memory state. We validate the effectiveness of our proposed designs on widely-used streaming video understanding datasets, i.e., OVO-Bench and StreamingBench, and the results showcase that our method performs better than other methods. Moreover, the advantage of our method becomes consistently evident even under high token usage drop rate settings, indicating the effectiveness and robustness of our method in unleashing the potential of the memory itself.

</details>


### [18] [Thread-Efficient Decoding for Neural Texture Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27888)
*Janarbek Matai,Sho Ikeda,Lukasz Lipski,Takahiro Harada*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种共享解码器MLP架构，结合逐级冻结训练策略和纹理聚类，将GPU线程发散降低25%-52%，同时保持渲染质量，在RX 9070 XT上实现最高8.48倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经纹理压缩（NTC）虽比BCn格式压缩比更高，但存在GPU线程发散问题，严重降低运行时性能，亟需解决。

Method: (1) 统一共享解码器架构，通过纹理分组降低发散；(2) 逐级冻结解码器的训练策略，提升稳定性与重建精度；(3) 基于CLIP嵌入的语义聚类策略，将相似纹理分组以有效共享解码器。

Result: 在500多个纹理和多个真实渲染场景上验证，线程发散降低25%-52%，在Radeon RX 9070 XT上对比非共享基线最高获得8.48倍加速，同时保持渲染质量。

Conclusion: 共享解码器架构配合语义聚类与渐进冻结训练，能显著缓解NTC的线程发散瓶颈，在保持渲染质量的前提下大幅提升运行时性能。

Abstract: Neural texture compression (NTC) achieves higher compression ratios than BCn formats but suffers from GPU thread divergence, which significantly reduces runtime performance. In this work, we propose a shared decoder MLP architecture -- trained with a gradual decoder freezing schedule -- combined with texture clustering to reduce thread divergence by 25%-52% while preserving rendering quality. We evaluate our method on over 500 textures and multiple real rendering scenes, demonstrating up to 8.48x speedup on the Radeon RX 9070 XT GPU compared to non-shared baselines. Our key contributions include: (1) a unified shared decoder architecture that reduces divergence by grouping textures; (2) a training recipe with gradual decoder freezing that improves stability and reconstruction accuracy; (3) a semantic clustering strategy using CLIP embeddings that groups similar textures for effective decoder sharing; and (4) comprehensive performance and ablation studies validating our approach.

</details>


### [19] [CommerceVibe: Learning to Design E-Commerce Creatives as Executable Visual Code via Dual-Feedback Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27893)
*Yajiao Xu,Jin Zhang,Jiangbo Ai,Tao Jiang,Mo Xu,Lina Huang,Chengfu Huo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CommerceVibe，将电商创意设计表示为可执行的HTML/CSS代码，通过条件程序合成进行生成，并引入双反馈强化学习（规则反馈+视觉语言模型反馈）来提升文本可读性、产品可见性、布局有效性及感知质量。在28000+样本上微调Qwen3.5-9B，基准测试中达到94.0/100分，显著优于SFT-only变体（87.3分）和外部模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散模型生成的电商创意是栅格图像，存在文字扭曲和产品细节不一致的问题，且缺乏显式结构导致难以编辑复用；复杂设计需求也难以编码为可验证的训练信号。

Method: 1) 将创意表示为可执行的HTML/CSS视觉代码，把生成任务建模为条件程序合成；2) 提出双反馈强化学习框架——规则反馈评估渲染程序（文本可读性、产品可见性、布局有效性），视觉反馈由VLM从六个感知与商业维度评估渲染创意；3) 在28000+电商样本上进行SFT，随后进行双反馈强化学习。

Result: 在1300个案例的基准上，CommerceVibe达到94.0/100的加权分数（SFT-only为87.3），超越多个强外部模型；五位电商设计专家的盲评进一步验证了改进效果。

Conclusion: CommerceVibe通过将生成任务转化为程序合成并结合互补的双反馈信号，实现了可控、可编辑、可扩展的电商创意生产，同时改善了约束满足和感知依赖质量。

Abstract: High-quality e-commerce creatives are essential for presenting products and conveying marketing messages. Recent diffusion models enable scalable creative generation and produce visually compelling images, but their flattened raster outputs often contain distorted text and inconsistent product details, requiring refinement before deployment. Moreover, without explicit structure, the resulting creatives are difficult to edit and reuse, while complex design requirements remain challenging to encode as verifiable training signals. To address these challenges, we present CommerceVibe, which represents creatives as executable visual code and formulates generation as conditional HTML/CSS program synthesis. Given product images, design requirements, and product information, it produces renderable, editable, and reusable creatives. We further introduce dual-feedback reinforcement learning, in which rule-based feedback evaluates rendered programs for text readability, product visibility, and layout validity, while visual feedback from a vision-language model (VLM) assesses rendered creatives against input specifications across six perceptual and commercial dimensions. Together, these complementary feedback signals improve both constraint satisfaction and perception-dependent quality. We perform supervised fine-tuning (SFT) of Qwen3.5-9B on over 28,000 e-commerce examples, followed by dual-feedback reinforcement learning. On a 1,300-case benchmark, the optimized CommerceVibe model achieves a weighted score of 94.0/100, compared with 87.3 for the SFT-only variant, and outperforms strong external models. Blind evaluations by five e-commerce design experts further validate these improvements. CommerceVibe supports controllable, editable, and scalable e-commerce creative production.

</details>


### [20] [DensityKV: Density-Guided KV Cache Compression for Long Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27922)
*Wenqu Zhao,Xuemin Chi,Xin Zhang,Guoqing Ma,Baorun Li,Jianjie Fang,Peizhi Tang,Chen Gao,Wei Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出DensityKV，一种无需训练的密度感知历史KV管理策略，通过Soft-Riesz密度测量post-RoPE键的局部冗余，限制历史状态重复积累，在相同KV容量下提升长时序视频生成的一致性与稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归视频扩散模型通过滑动窗口注意力实现流式生成，但生成块会递归传播外观和运动误差。历史KV记忆可保持长时序一致性，但无限积累的KV存档导致持续增长的存储开销和冗余覆盖。

Method: DensityKV为每个注意力头维护独立的token级KV库，使用Soft-Riesz密度度量post-RoPE键之间的局部冗余，并通过约束邻域密度增长来限制历史状态重复积累，同时保留每个完成生成块中的一致状态。

Result: 在三个自回归视频生成骨干网络和多种生成长度下，在相同历史KV容量上限时，DensityKV提升了长时序一致性和生成稳定性，并将历史存储上限与生成长度解耦。

Conclusion: DensityKV提供了一种轻量、无需训练的历史KV银行管理方案，有效平衡了历史记忆保留与存储开销，为长视频自回归生成提供可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Autoregressive video diffusion models enable streaming generation through sliding-window attention, but each generated block is conditioned on previously generated content, causing appearance and motion errors to propagate recursively over time. Historical key-value (KV) memory preserves earlier subject and scene states and helps maintain long-horizon consistency. However, retaining every generated state creates a historical archive that grows continuously with the rollout, while recurrent states repeatedly add redundant coverage. To address this problem, we propose DensityKV, a training-free historical KV bank management strategy. DensityKV maintains a separate token-level KV bank for each attention head and measures local redundancy among the post-RoPE keys that directly parameterize attention routing using Soft-Riesz density. By constraining neighborhood-density growth after states enter the bank, DensityKV limits repeated historical accumulation while preserving coherent states from each completed generation block. Experiments across three autoregressive video generation backbones and multiple generation lengths show that, at the same upper bound on historical KV capacity, DensityKV improves long-horizon consistency and generation stability while keeping persistent historical storage bounded independently of rollout length.

</details>


### [21] [PCBnet: A Dataset and Automatic Construction of SPICE Netlists from Schematic Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27923)
*Zhen Huang,Yuhao Gao,Yuzhi Liu,Daian Cheng,Chengyuan Shao,Yucheng Chen,Yongjian Jia,Futing Zhang,Yichen Shi,Wenhao Wang,Zuyan He,Yangbo Wei,Zhanfei Chen,Jinlong Yan,Yu Zhang,Haoying Wu,Ting-Jung Lin,Lei He*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出PCBnet，一个包含300多个真实PCB原理图设计、配对的SPICE网表数据集，并开发了结合视觉识别、拓扑构建和多智能体纠错的自动化原理图到网表转换流程，实现94.54%的元件检测mAP和84.47%的端到端连接正确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PCB设计自动化受限于缺乏大规模的配对原理图-网表数据集，且PCB原理图因元件类型多样、布线拓扑复杂和文本标注嘈杂而难以处理。

Method: 提出自动化原理图到网表转换流程，结合视觉识别、拓扑构建和基于领域知识的多智能体纠错机制。

Result: 元件检测mAP达94.54%，文本识别准确率98.57%，端到端连接正确率84.47%。数据集包含超过50,000个元件实例、150,000条导线、100,000个文本区域和400,000个字符。

Conclusion: PCBnet为未来AI驱动的PCB设计自动化提供了基准测试平台和数据基础。

Abstract: Printed circuit boards (PCBs) are fundamental to modern electronic systems, yet AI-driven PCB design automation remains constrained by the lack of large-scale paired schematic-netlist datasets. PCB schematics are particularly challenging due to diverse component types, complex wiring topologies, and noisy textual annotations. To address this gap, we present PCBnet, a large-scale PCB schematic dataset comprising over 300 real-world designs with annotated pins and paired SPICE netlists. It contains more than 50,000 component instances, 150,000 wires, 100,000 text regions, and 400,000 characters. We further develop an automated schematic-to-netlist pipeline that combines visual recognition, topology construction, and domain-knowledge-guided multi-agent correction. The proposed method achieves 94.54% component detection mAP, 98.57% text recognition accuracy, and 84.47% end-to-end connectivity accuracy. PCBnet provides a benchmark and data foundation for future AI-driven PCB design automation.

</details>


### [22] [Training-Free Temporal Abstraction for General Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27929)
*Etienne Casanova,Sevan Brodjian,Pietro Perona*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出STITCH，一种无需训练的通用视频时间分割方法，利用预训练视频-文本模型的嵌入序列变化，将视频划分为语义上有意义的时间块，适用于事件边界检测、基于语言的时刻检索和长视频VLM帧选择等多种下游任务，且在少量帧/标记限制下表现尤为突出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 逐帧分析视频成本高昂，且现有方法通常针对单一任务（事件边界检测、时刻检索、帧选择）分别训练专用模型或使用专用架构，缺乏统一的、可复用的时间结构表示。作者希望探究预训练视频-文本模型能否提供足够的时序结构来同时支持多个下游任务。

Method: STITCH采用训练无关（training-free）的方法：首先使用冻结的视频-文本骨干网络对短视频窗口进行嵌入，然后检测嵌入序列中的变化点，据此将视频划分为语义上一致的时间块。这些块仅需对每个视频计算一次，便可在多个任务中复用。

Result: 在通用事件边界检测、基于语言的时刻检索和长视频VLM推理的帧选择三项任务上，STITCH无需任何任务特定训练即可与更专用的方法保持竞争力，尤其在可处理帧数或标记数受限的情况下优势明显。

Conclusion: 可复用的时间抽象是通用视频理解的一个有前景的研究方向——密集视频流可以被一次性转换为语义单元，供下游系统进行定位、检索、采样或推理。

Abstract: Videos are expensive to analyze frame by frame, yet many video understanding tasks depend on knowing where relevant moments occur. A system may need to find when an action changes, locate the segment described by a sentence, or choose a few frames for a vision-language model. Existing methods often solve these problems separately, using task-specific training data or specialized architectures. We study whether a pretrained video-text model can provide enough temporal structure to support several of these tasks at once. We present STITCH, a training-free method that divides a video into semantically meaningful temporal chunks. STITCH embeds short video windows with a frozen video-text backbone and detects changes in the resulting embedding sequence. These chunks are computed once per video and reused across tasks. We evaluate STITCH on generic event boundary detection, language-based moment retrieval, and frame selection for long-video VLM reasoning. Across all three settings, STITCH remains competitive with more specialized methods while requiring no task-specific training, with especially clear gains when only a small number of frames or tokens can be processed. These results suggest that reusable temporal abstraction is a promising direction for general video understanding, allowing dense video streams to be converted once into semantic units that can be localized, retrieved, sampled, or reasoned over by downstream systems.

</details>


### [23] [GAAT: Geometry-Aware Alignment Transformer for Multimodal UAV Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27971)
*Jingpu Yang,Debin Tang,Yilin Sun,Fengxian Ji,Jiahua Zhu,Wenrui Ding,Yufeng Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出GAAT（几何感知对齐Transformer），一种面向无人机多模态感知的对齐优先预训练模型。GAAT通过syncPATC学习同步视图变换下的patch中心一致性，生成几何先验（token/query置信度、query中心、子token偏移），并利用MG-Sparse-MMA实现基于几何校准的稀疏融合，配合RA-QCGCL对比学习分支。作者还构建了UAVMeta和StateBench基准，在六个下游任务上验证了GAAT的优异迁移性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机多模态感知（RGB、IR、SAR、深度）面临视差、平台运动和镜头畸变导致的跨模态patch中心偏移问题，这削弱了密集对比学习和跨模态融合所依赖的空间对应关系。现有系统通常只能做到全局或图像中心对齐，无法处理局部残余错位。

Method: 1) syncPATC：在无对应标注情况下学习同步视图变换下的patch中心一致性，输出几何先验。2) MG-Sparse-MMA：基于几何先验在top-K个可靠区域进行query介导的稀疏融合，替代密集全patch交互。3) RA-QCGCL：通过可靠patch-to-patch、patch-to-query和query-to-query三个对比分支将预训练监督与稀疏query瓶颈对齐。4) UAVMeta和StateBench：提供四项采集状态评分（相机可靠性、观测尺度、视角稳定性、飞行机动复杂度）。

Result: 在六个下游任务上的大量实验表明GAAT具有一致的优越迁移性能，成为无人机感知的SOTA多模态基础模型。StateBench还能对真实采集条件进行系统性诊断。

Conclusion: GAAT证明了对齐优先的策略和多模态多模态预训练中残余错位的有效处理，为无人机多模态感知提供了全新的基础模型范式。

Abstract: Unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV) multimodal perception integrates visible (RGB), infrared (IR), synthetic aperture radar (SAR), and depth sensors for scene understanding under diverse conditions. However, differences in optics, resolution, and mounting often limit practical systems to global or image-center alignment. After tokenization, parallax, platform motion, and lens distortion can shift corresponding patch centers across modalities, weakening the spatial correspondence assumed by dense contrastive learning and cross-modal fusion. We propose GAAT (Geometry-Aware Alignment Transformer), an alignment-first pretrained model that estimates local correspondence reliability before cross-modal interaction. GAAT introduces syncPATC, which learns patch-center consistency under synchronized view transformations without correspondence annotations. It emits geometric priors, including token and query confidence, query centers, and sub-token offsets, that identify reliable local anchors across residual misalignment. Guided by these priors, MG-Sparse-MMA performs query-mediated sparse fusion over top-K_s reliable regions, replacing dense all-patch interaction with geometry-calibrated local updates. RA-QCGCL aligns pretraining supervision with this sparse query bottleneck through reliable patch-to-patch, patch-to-query, and query-to-query contrastive branches. We introduce UAVMeta and StateBench, which provide four acquisition-state scores derived from platform telemetry and image statistics: camera reliability, observation scale, viewpoint stability, and flight maneuver complexity. Extensive experiments across six downstream tasks demonstrate consistently superior transfer performance, establishing GAAT as a state-of-the-art multimodal foundation model for UAV perception. StateBench further enables a systematic diagnosis of real-world acquisition conditions.

</details>


### [24] [GAN-Based Semantic Communication for Image Transmission in IoV](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27989)
*Ruixing Ren,Shan Chen,Junhui Zhao,Xiaoke Sun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于生成对抗网络的语义通信框架，用于车联网中的协同感知，通过金字塔注意力网络提取语义标签图，并引入语义优先级保留机制，在接收端利用粗到细的多分辨率生成器和多尺度判别器实现高保真图像重建，在Cityscapes数据集上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统通信系统在有限带宽和动态信道条件下传输视觉数据时存在效率和保真度瓶颈，需要更高效的语义通信方案来满足车联网协同感知的需求。

Method: 发送端采用金字塔注意力网络提取语义标签图，并引入基于驾驶安全的语义优先级保留机制来差异化分配权重、指导比特分配和损失函数设计；接收端设计集成粗到细多分辨率生成器和多尺度判别器的图像重建模块，结合时间一致性分支、空间金字塔池化和类感知卷积层实现高质量图像重建；训练时联合使用对抗损失、特征匹配损失和感知损失。

Result: 在Cityscapes数据集上的实验表明，该方法在语义分割准确率和重建图像质量上均优于现有方法，并且在AWGN和Rayleigh信道下保持稳定的重建性能。

Conclusion: 所提出的GAN-based语义通信框架有效解决了车联网协同感知中视觉数据传输的效率和保真度瓶颈，在动态信道条件下表现出鲁棒性。

Abstract: For cooperative perception in the internet of vehicles, this paper proposes a generative adversarial network-based semantic communication framework to address the efficiency and fidelity bottlenecks of traditional communication systems in visual data transmission under limited bandwidth and dynamic channel conditions. At the transmitter, the framework adopts a pyramid attention network to extract semantic label maps and introduces a semantic priority preservation mechanism. It assigns differentiated weights to distinct semantic categories based on driving safety, guiding bit allocation and loss function design. At the receiver, an image reconstruction module integrating a coarse to-fine multi-resolution generator and multi-scale discriminator is designed. Combined with the temporal consistency branch, spatial pyramid pooling and class-aware convolutional layers, it achieves high-fidelity reconstruction of high-quality images from corrupted semantic labels. The model is trained with combined adversarial, feature matching and perceptual losses, effectively improving semantic consistency and visual realism of generated images. Experimental results on the Cityscapes dataset show that the proposed method outperforms existing counterparts in both semantic segmentation accuracy and reconstructed image quality, and maintains stable reconstruction performance under AWGN and Rayleigh channels.

</details>


### [25] [A-PAIR: A Benchmark and Identity-Consistent Grounding Framework for Air-Ground Cross-View Referring Person Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27997)
*Zhoupeng Guo,Xinjie Yao,Yunqi Zhu,Zhihe Fan,Siqi Zhao,Jianjun Chen,Yichen Dong,Yan Fan,Pengfei Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文首次提出并研究了空地跨视角指代行人检测问题（AGCV-RPD），构建了包含22,137个跨视角指代样本的综合基准A-PAIR，并提出ICRG框架通过分解式指代定位、候选完整性监督和跨视角一致性校准实现空地联合行人匹配，将pair F1从16.65%提升至22.28%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有指代表达理解和开放词汇接地方法未能联合考虑跨视角身份一致性，在空地协同场景（如相似行人干扰、弱空中外观线索）下不够充分，亟需一个系统的基准和方法来解决空地跨视角指代行人检测问题。

Method: 提出FARA半自动标注框架（分解式标注与指代对齐）用于高效构建A-PAIR基准；提出ICRG框架（身份一致指代接地），包含分解式指代接地、候选完整性监督和跨视角一致性校准三个模块，实现空地成对联合检测。

Result: ICRG在ground、aerial和pair三个层级均优于强基线，将pair F1从16.65%提升至22.28%，验证了方法在跨视角身份一致推理上的有效性。

Conclusion: AGCV-RPD需要成对检测和身份一致推理，空地跨视角指代行人检测是一个独特且具有挑战性的问题，需要专门的基准和方法来解决。

Abstract: Air-ground cross-view referring person detection is a necessary component in the language-to-perception-to-control chain of collective embodied intelligence, grounding a language command into the same physical target before ground and aerial agents can coordinate downstream actions. Existing referring expression comprehension and open-vocabulary grounding methods do not jointly account for cross-view identity consistency, making them insufficient for Air-Ground Cross-View Referring Person Detection (AGCV-RPD), which involves similar pedestrian distractors, weak aerial appearance cues, and cross-view identity consistency. To study this problem, we introduce Air-Ground Paired Identity-Aware Referring (A-PAIR), the first comprehensive AGCV-RPD benchmark, containing 22,137 cross-view referring samples. To construct A-PAIR efficiently, we propose Factorized Annotation and Referential Alignment (FARA), a semi-automatic annotation framework that generates factorized referring descriptions and identity-consistency supervision at reduced cost. We propose Identity-Consistent Referring Grounding (ICRG), a framework that combines factorized referential grounding, candidate-completeness supervision, and cross-view consistency calibration for joint air-ground pair selection. ICRG improves ground, aerial, and pair-level detection over strong baselines, increasing pair F1 from 16.65% to 22.28%. These results show that AGCV-RPD requires paired detection and identity-consistent reasoning.

</details>


### [26] [Visual Token Coding for Video Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28008)
*Chenxin Fang,Tao Chen,JunChao You,Jun Peng,Yiyi Zhou,Rongrong Ji*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的视频多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）令牌压缩范式——视觉令牌编码（VTC），借鉴经典视频编码原理（如HEVC）对视频令牌进行结构化压缩，并通过动态分辨率输入、动态令牌分配和空间覆盖Top-K等策略进一步优化，在仅使用50%令牌预算时保持100.1%的平均性能，降至25%预算时仍能保留97.8%的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频多模态大语言模型在处理视频时会产生大量视觉令牌，导致计算开销巨大。现有令牌压缩方法缺乏对视频内容语义结构的深入利用，压缩效率与性能保持之间存在矛盾，亟需一种更高效、更智能的令牌压缩范式。

Method: 受视频编码标准（如HEVC）中I/P帧预测与残差计算的启发，VTC通过预测视频的I/P帧并计算帧间残差来估计令牌冗余，实现结构化压缩。在此基础上，引入动态分辨率输入（DyRSO）、动态令牌分配（DyTA）和空间覆盖Top-K（SC-TopK）等动态设计，形成增强版本$VTC_{Dy}$。该方法作为即插即用模块，无需对MLLM进行额外微调。

Result: 将VTC应用于三个MLLM并在多个视频理解基准上进行实验。结果显示，$VTC_{Dy}$在Qwen3-VL上使用50%令牌预算时平均性能保留率达到100.1%，即使在令牌预算减少至25%时仍能保持97.8%的平均性能。

Conclusion: VTC作为一种无需重新训练MLLM的即插即用令牌压缩方法，能够在大幅降低令牌开销的同时几乎不损失甚至略微提升模型性能，为视频MLLM的高效推理提供了新的解决思路，并将代码开源以便社区使用和进一步研究。

Abstract: In this paper, we propose a new token compression paradigm for video Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs), termed Visual Token Coding (VTC). Inspired by classical video coding principles, e.g., HEVC, VTC performs structured compression by predicting the I/P frames of a video and measuring their frame-wise residuals to estimate token redundancy. Based on this baseline framework, we also enhance VTC with a set of novel dynamic designs, such as Dynamic Resolution Input (DyRSO), Dynamic Token Allocation (DyTA), and Spatial Coverage Top-K (SC-TopK), and term this new approach $VTC_{Dy}$. To validate VTC, we apply it to three MLLMs and conduct experiments on multiple video understanding benchmarks. The experimental results show that VTC$_{\mathrm{Dy}}$ achieves an average performance retention of 100.1% with a 50% token budget for Qwen3-VL, while still retaining 97.8% of the average performance when the token budget is reduced to 25%. Moreover, as a plug-and-play design, VTC requires no additional tuning of MLLMs for token coding. Our code is available at https://github.com/Msr233/VTC.

</details>


### [27] [3D-USE: From Image-Level to Scene-Level Underwater Enhancement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28020)
*Jieyu Yuan,Yuanlin Zhang,Jihong Li,Chunle Guo,Huimin Lu,Chongyi Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出3D-USE框架，用于水下场景级增强（USE），通过学习持久且可见性增强的3D场景表示，实现从退化水下多视角观测中生成一致增强的渲染。框架包含MediumRBF（基于径向基锚点的介质感知高斯场景）和ATC（外观过渡一致性）+ U-BAF（水下双边外观场）两个阶段，无需配对的增强3D数据即可实现一致的场景增强渲染。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水下3D重建会忠实再现采集视图的颜色偏移和可见性损失，而物理反演方法可能在恢复场景外观时留下估计误差。现有方法缺乏既能提供可靠场景表示用于增强、又能提供一致的增强目标（无需配对增强3D数据）的方案，因此需要一种新的场景级增强方法。

Method: 提出两阶段框架3D-USE。第一阶段，MediumRBF构建介质感知高斯场景，通过共享径向基锚点表示水效，显式分解物体与介质贡献；第二阶段，ATC将配对2D水下图像增强（UIE）知识迁移到场景全局和高斯局部目标，避免不一致增强视图的直接监督，U-BAF在辐射度和介质外观上实现这些增强目标。推理时场景可直接渲染增强新视图，无需2D UIE模型。

Result: 在真实水下场景上实验表明，该方法在提升可见性和跨视图一致性的同时，保持了重建质量。

Conclusion: 3D-USE通过将2D增强知识迁移到3D场景表示，成功实现了水下场景级一致增强渲染，为水下3D重建与增强提供了一种无需配对3D数据的有效方案。

Abstract: Underwater 3D reconstruction faithfully reproduces the color shifts and visibility loss of captured views, while physical inversion may leave estimation errors in the recovered scene appearance. We formulate Underwater Scene-level Enhancement (USE) as learning a persistent, visibility-enhanced 3D scene representation from degraded multi-view underwater observations, enabling consistent enhanced rendering. Realizing USE requires both a reliable scene representation for enhancement and a consistent enhancement target without paired enhanced 3D data. Therefore, we present 3D-USE, a two-stage framework. First, the Medium Radial Basis Anchor Representation (MediumRBF) establishes a medium-aware Gaussian scene by representing water effects with shared radial-basis anchors and explicitly decomposing object and medium contributions. Based on this fixed scene representation, Appearance Transition Consensus (ATC) transfers paired 2D underwater image enhancement (UIE) knowledge into scene-global and Gaussian-local targets, avoiding direct supervision from inconsistent enhanced views. An Underwater Bilateral Appearance Field (U-BAF) then realizes these targets in Gaussian radiance and medium appearance. The scene directly renders enhanced novel views without a 2D UIE model at inference. Experiments on real underwater scenes show improved visibility and cross-view consistency while preserving reconstruction quality.

</details>


### [28] [ZipMVS: Multi-View Stereo with Compressed Cost Volumes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28033)
*Guanglin Jin,Hongshan Yu,Javier Civera,Zhaoxin Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ZipMVS是一种针对资源受限场景（如航空航天、自主系统）设计的高效多视图立体（MVS）重建方法，通过新颖的深度假设策略大幅压缩成本体积，从而显著降低GPU内存消耗，同时在DTU和Tanks and Temples数据集上保持了与现有高效MVS方法相当的重建质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MVS方法虽然能通过多视角RGB图像间的几何约束实现高精度3D重建，但其巨大的内存需求严重阻碍了在航空航天和自主系统等资源敏感领域的部署应用。

Method: 提出一种新颖的深度假设策略，通过压缩成本体积（cost volume）来显著降低GPU内存消耗，同时保持重建精度不损失。该方法在深度假设层面进行优化，实现内存效率和重建质量之间的平衡。

Result: 在DTU和Tanks and Temples数据集上的实验表明，ZipMVS与其他面向效率的MVS方法相比，达到了具有竞争力的重建质量，并在重建质量与GPU内存使用之间取得了良好的平衡。代码已开源。

Conclusion: ZipMVS证明了通过巧妙的深度假设设计可以在不牺牲重建质量的前提下大幅压缩成本体积内存，为MVS方法在资源受限平台上部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Multi-view stereo (MVS) methods typically deliver highly accurate 3D reconstructions from multiple registered RGB images, thanks to the highly informative, geometric constraints between them. However, their substantial memory requirements remain a major obstacle for deployment in domains such as aerospace and autonomous systems, where resource efficiency is critical. In this work, we introduce ZipMVS, an MVS method specifically designed for efficient high-quality reconstruction. We propose a novel depth-hypothesis strategy that enables substantial compression of the cost volume, hence greatly reducing GPU memory consumption while preserving reconstruction accuracy. Experiments on the DTU and Tanks and Temples datasets show that ZipMVS achieves competitive reconstruction quality compared with other efficiency-oriented MVS methods, while achieving a competitive balance between reconstruction quality and GPU memory usage. The code is available at https://github.com/JihnGlyn/ZipMVS

</details>


### [29] [Dynamic Alignment Compensation for Hallucination Mitigation in Large Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28058)
*Kairong Yu,Zixin Zhu,Le Yu,Hongwei Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无需训练的推理时方法——动态对齐补偿（DAC），通过检测跨模态表征在解码器层间和生成步骤间的退化与漂移，并施加轻量级残差补偿来减少LVLM的幻觉输出。在九个多模态基准上的实验表明DAC能持续降低幻觉同时保持整体性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的大视觉语言模型（LVLMs）幻觉缓解方法主要依赖外部监督、输出校准或注意力调节，忽略了自回归生成过程中的内部表征动力学。作者识别出推理时的失败模式：跨模态表征在解码器层间退化并在生成步骤间漂移，这会破坏token预测的稳定性并增加幻觉风险。

Method: 提出Dynamic Alignment Compensation (DAC)，一种无需训练的推理时方法。DAC包含两个组件：1）Layer-wise Semantic Compensation（层间语义补偿），用于缓解跨层表征退化；2）Sequential Semantic Correction（时序语义修正），用于约束生成步骤间的时域漂移。该方法通过检测表征发散并选择性地施加轻量级残差补偿来实现。

Result: 在九个幻觉专项及通用多模态基准上，使用多个LVLM骨干网络进行实验，结果表明DAC能一致性地减少幻觉，同时保持强劲的整体性能。

Conclusion: DAC验证了通过干预自回归生成的内部表征动力学可以有效缓解幻觉，且无需额外训练，为LVLM幻觉缓解提供了新的推理时方案。

Abstract: Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs) remain prone to hallucinations, producing responses that are irrelevant or inconsistent with the multimodal input. Existing mitigation methods mainly rely on external supervision, output calibration, or attention regulation, leaving the internal representation dynamics of autoregressive generation underexplored. We identify an inference-time failure mode in which cross-modal representations degrade across decoder layers and drift across generation steps, destabilizing token prediction and increasing hallucination risk. We propose \emph{Dynamic Alignment Compensation} (DAC), a training-free inference-time method that detects representation divergence and selectively applies lightweight residual compensation. DAC combines Layer-wise Semantic Compensation to mitigate inter-layer degradation with Sequential Semantic Correction to constrain temporal drift. Experiments on nine hallucination-focused and general-purpose multimodal benchmarks across multiple LVLM backbones show that DAC consistently reduces hallucinations while maintaining strong overall performance.

</details>


### [30] [A Controlled Audit of Architectural Complexity in Uncertainty-Aware Multi-Organ Ultrasound Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28063)
*Yang Song,Pengbo Sun,Shichang Feng,Ye Zhu,Xin Xu,Ziran Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文对多器官超声分类器中复杂的注意力、混合专家路由、不确定性门控及证据深度学习（EDL）等组件进行了受控复杂度审计。通过在两个数据集、十组匹配种子及OOD否决机制下的系统性比较，发现完整的EDL模型（Full-EDL）并未在宏F1上显著优于简化方案，而简单交叉熵加温度缩放（Simple-CE+TS）在校准负对数似然和选择性风险排序上表现更优；EDL的原始校准优势经温度缩放后消失。最终选择Simple-CE+TS用于分布内目标，同时保留Full-EDL作为最大参考，强调组件应通过功能性和重训练证据获得保留资格。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前多器官超声分类器趋向于堆叠注意力、混合专家、不确定性门控和证据学习等复杂组件，但缺乏严格的实证证据表明这些复杂化为模型带来实际性能增益。研究者希望建立一个受控的复杂度审计框架来判断"多即好"假设是否成立，为部署决策提供基于证据的选择依据。

Method: 构建受控复杂度审计框架：对比6个候选模型（主数据集）和3个内部复现模型，使用10组匹配随机种子、冻结图像级划分、容量与优化感知比较、对称温度缩放、配对决策规则以及独立的OOD否决测试。系统性地从宏F1、校准NLL、选择性风险排序和OOD鲁棒性等多个维度评估Full-EDL及其简化替代方案。

Result: Full-EDL在两个数据集上均未建立可靠的宏F1增益；简化替代方案在非劣效性边际下仍不确定。Simple-CE+TS在两个数据集上满足校准NLL标准并展示有利的选择性风险排序。EDL的原始校准优势经温度缩放后完全消失。门控在审计检查点上影响可忽略不计，删除Full-only链条无稳定收益。Simple-CE对胎儿探头触发OOD否决但肺部探头未触发，无法做出无条件OOD安全声明。

Conclusion: 复杂的EDL组件并未带来经证实的性能增益，应优先选择经过校准的Simple-CE+TS用于分布内评估目标，Full-EDL仅保留为最大参考。组件的保留必须通过功能性和重训练证据来证明，且校准评估与分布偏移可靠性应分开独立评估。该框架为深度学习组件选择的证据驱动方法提供了示范。

Abstract: Multi-organ ultrasound classifiers increasingly combine attention, mixture-of-experts routing, uncertainty gating, and evidential deep learning (EDL) objectives to address heterogeneous anatomy and acquisition. Yet a plausible design rationale does not by itself establish that an added component improves the trained system. We contribute a controlled complexity-audit framework, applied to the deployment decision between the maximal evidential candidate Full-EDL and simpler alternatives. Six candidates were evaluated on the primary dataset and three in an internal replication, using ten matched seeds, frozen image-level partitions, capacity- and optimisation-aware comparisons, symmetric temperature scaling, paired decision rules, and a separate out-of-distribution (OOD) veto. Retaining Full-EDL did not establish a reliable macro-F1 gain on either dataset, while the simplified alternatives remained inconclusive under the non-inferiority margin. Simple cross-entropy with temperature scaling (Simple-CE+TS) met the calibrated negative log-likelihood criterion on both datasets and showed favourable selective-risk ordering. The raw calibration advantage of evidential training disappeared after temperature scaling and did not recur on the second dataset. The gate had negligible observable influence at the audited checkpoints, and deleting the Full-only chain revealed no stable task or calibrated-loss benefit. Simple-CE nevertheless triggered the OOD veto against the fetal probe but not the lung probe, precluding an unconditional OOD-safety claim. We therefore selected Simple-CE+TS for the evaluated in-distribution objective while retaining Full-EDL as the maximal reference. Components should earn retention through functional and retraining-based evidence, and calibration and distribution-shift reliability should be evaluated separately.

</details>


### [31] [VersaGauss: A Versatile Framework for Generating Multiphase Dynamics with 3D Gaussians](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28069)
*Ruijie Su,Lingxiao Yang,Xiaohua Xie,Jianhuang Lai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VersaGauss是一个统一框架，仅需少量图像输入即可生成、模拟和渲染支持多相物态交互的物理驱动3D动态场景，涵盖流体、橡胶、沙子、雪等多种材质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于3D高斯的物理动态生成方法主要针对固体对象，仅能处理单相碰撞交互，缺乏对多相物态相互作用的支持。

Method: 提出VersaGauss统一框架，包含粒子剪枝算法优化高斯核分布、耦合多相点方法（CMPM）建模多相交互，以及CMPM中的谐波插值和高斯演化策略实现逼真流体渲染。

Result: 大量实验表明该框架能够模拟流体、橡胶、沙子、雪等多种材质之间的相互作用，且代码已开源。

Conclusion: VersaGauss成功实现了多相物态交互的物理驱动3D动态场景生成与渲染，扩展了3D高斯表示在物理仿真中的应用范围。

Abstract: Recent progress has been made in 3D Gaussian representation for reconstruction, generation, and physical simulation. However, current approaches mainly concentrate on physics-based dynamic generation of solid objects and only handle single-phase collision interactions. We introduce VersaGauss, a unified framework for generation, simulation, and rendering that supports versatile physics-based dynamic generation, particularly for multiphase interactions. Our system takes a few images as input and produces a realistic, physics-driven 3D dynamic scene with multiple objects. To optimize the Gaussian kernel distribution, we develop a particle pruning algorithm. We also propose the Coupled Multiphase Point Method (CMPM) to effectively model and generate multiphase interactions. Additionally, harmonic interpolation within CMPM and a Gaussian evolution strategy are introduced to achieve realistic fluid rendering. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our framework can simulate interactions among various materials such as fluid, rubber, sand, snow, and others. Code is available at https://github.com/Elowen-surj/VersaGauss.

</details>


### [32] [CF-YOLO: Context-Aware Feature Refinement for Camouflaged Industrial Micro-Defect Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28070)
*Xinda Yu,Kunxin Zheng,Chunan Yu,Qingbo Song,Hao Xiao,Ying Zang,Jie Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向工业微小缺陷检测的实时检测框架CF-YOLO，通过上下文感知聚合模块(CPAM)和特征加性细化模块(FARM)分别解决背景伪装和弱特征表示问题，并构建了包含1,847张图像和4,898个缺陷实例的铜管缺陷数据集(CTDD)。实验表明该方法在mAP@50和Precision上分别超越YOLOv11达2.2%和3.9%，同时保持实时推理速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业部件（如铜管）表面微小缺陷自动检测对质量保障至关重要，但缺陷尺度极小且与复杂背景视觉伪装，导致特征表示弱、误检率和漏检率高，现有方法难以有效解决这一问题。

Method: 提出CF-YOLO框架，包含两个核心模块：(1) 上下文感知聚合模块(CPAM)，协同大核感知宏纹理上下文和小核聚合锐利边界，打破背景伪装；(2) 特征加性细化模块(FARM)，采用线性复杂度加性token混合器全局验证和细化细粒度异常表示，抑制噪声误差。同时构建了铜管缺陷数据集(CTDD)。

Result: 在CTDD数据集上，该方法优于包括YOLOv11在内的代表性基线检测器，mAP@50提升2.2%，Precision提升3.9%，且保持实时推理速度。

Conclusion: 该工作为高精度工业检测提供了鲁棒高效的解决方案，弥合了上下文理解与细粒度特征分析之间的差距，代码和模型已开源。

Abstract: Automated detection of surface micro-defects on industrial components, such as copper tubes, is critically important for quality assurance but remains challenging due to the minute scale of anomalies and their visual camouflage against complex backgrounds. These factors lead to weak feature representations and high rates of false positives and missed detections. To address these issues, we propose a novel real-time detection framework designed for efficient context perception and feature refinement. Our method integrates a Context-Perception Aggregation Module (CPAM), which synergises large-kernel perception for macro-texture context and small-kernel aggregation for sharp boundary delineation, effectively breaking the background camouflage. Furthermore, a Feature Additive Refinement Module (FARM) employs a linear-complexity additive token mixer to globally verify and refine the representation of fine-grained anomalies, suppressing noise-induced errors. To support research in this domain, we introduce the Copper Tube Defect Dataset (CTDD), a manually annotated benchmark containing 1,847 images and 4,898 boundingbox defect instances from copper-tube inspection scenarios. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our detector achieves strong and consistent performance on CTDD, outperforming representative baseline detectors, including YOLOv11, by 2.2% in mAP@50 and 3.9% in Precision while maintaining real-time inference speed. This work provides a robust and efficient solution for high-precision industrial inspection, bridging the gap between contextual understanding and detailed feature analysis. Our code and model are available at: https://github.com/Yu-Xinda/CFYOLO-Context-Aware-Feature-Refinement-for-Camouflaged-Industrial-Micro-Defect-Detection

</details>


### [33] [Task-State Adaptation with Prototype Memory for Multi-Task Dense Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28078)
*Yangyang Xu,Haobo Yuan,Yuzhu Wang,Duo Su,Xi Ye,Yibo Yang,Jun Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MemMTL多任务密集预测框架，通过任务状态估计和可学习原型记忆实现任务条件专家路由，包含任务无关残差银行和稀疏Top-k专家选择，但当前草稿中的数值结果尚未基于规范实现重新生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉基础骨干网络为密集预测提供了强表征，但单个共享特征仍需要支持具有不同且依赖图像的自适应需求的多任务，因此需要更灵活的任务自适应机制。

Method: MemMTL框架从全局视觉上下文估计紧凑任务状态，通过可学习任务状态原型记忆精炼该状态，将精炼状态转换为任务条件专家logit并与token级logit结合，在共享的局部专家银行上进行稀疏Top-k选择；另设任务无关残差银行提供共同自适应路径，两条路径在骨干特征上各加一次后用于任务特定预测。

Result: 在NYUD-v2和PASCAL-Context上使用SAM 3和ViT-L骨干网络，定义了匹配的评估协议来测量预测质量、计算成本以及任务状态条件化、原型检索和稀疏路由的贡献。但当前工作草稿中的数值记录早于规范实现，需重新生成以支持实证主张。

Conclusion: MemMTL通过任务状态驱动的稀疏专家路由和任务无关残差路径相结合，为多任务密集预测提供灵活的自适应框架，但实证结论需待数字重新生成后才能确立。

Abstract: Vision foundation backbones provide strong representations for dense prediction, yet a single shared feature still needs to support tasks with different, image-dependent adaptation requirements. We propose MemMTL, a multi-task dense prediction framework that estimates a compact task state from global visual context and refines it through a learnable task-state prototype memory. The refined state is converted into task-conditioned expert logits and combined with token-level logits before sparse top-$k$ selection over a local expert bank shared by all tasks. A separate task-agnostic residual bank provides a common adaptation path, and both paths are added once to the backbone feature before task-specific prediction. We specify a matched evaluation protocol on NYUD-v2 and PASCAL-Context with SAM 3 and ViT-L backbones to measure predictive quality, computational cost, and the contributions of task-state conditioning, prototype retrieval, and sparse routing. The numerical record in the present working draft predates this canonical implementation and must be regenerated before it can support empirical claims.

</details>


### [34] [Cyc3D: Evaluating Cyclic Structural Stability and Asset Usability in Image-to-3D Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28080)
*Liwen Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Cyc3D基准，从跨视角对象一致性和表示质量两个维度评估图像到3D生成模型，通过闭环渲染-再生成-对齐协议量化几何、感知和语义漂移，并评估网格离散化、UV参数化等资产可用性指标。实验发现闭源前馈模型优于开源优化基线，但最强方法周期稳定性分数仍低于48，表明视觉合理生成与稳健3D理解之间仍存在显著差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图像条件3D生成评估协议主要关注渲染视图的合理性和语义对齐，忽略了生成器是否形成稳定的3D解释以及生成资产能否在图形管线中实际使用。需要一个多维度基准来全面评估这些被忽视的关键方面。

Method: 提出Cyc3D多维基准，在资产生层面测量跨视角对象一致性；在模型层面提出View-Cycle Structural Consistency，即闭环渲染-再生成-对齐协议，从新颖视角反复重新观察生成资产并量化几何、感知和语义漂移；同时评估几何结构、参考图像保真度、网格离散化与效率、UV参数化质量。

Result: 在五个代表性图像到3D系统上的实验显示，闭源前馈模型在几何保真度、网格质量和周期稳定性方面一致优于开源优化基线。然而即使最强的方法周期稳定性分数也低于48，揭示了视觉合理生成与稳健3D对象理解之间的持续差距。

Conclusion: Cyc3D诊断能够暴露单一感知评分所掩盖的失败模式，为模型不稳定性和表示缺陷提供可解释的证据，表明当前图像到3D生成虽有视觉进步但仍缺乏稳健的3D对象理解。

Abstract: Image-conditioned 3D generation has advanced rapidly, yet existing evaluation protocols largely judge rendered-view plausibility and semantic alignment, overlooking whether a generator forms a stable 3D interpretation and produces assets usable in graphics pipelines. We introduce Cyc3D, a multidimensional benchmark that evaluates image-to-3D generation along two complementary axes: Cross-View Object Consistency and Representation Quality. At the asset level, Cyc3D measures whether object identity remains semantically coherent across rendered viewpoints. At the model level, we propose View-Cycle Structural Consistency, a closed-loop render-regenerate-align protocol that repeatedly re-observes a generated asset from novel views and quantifies geometric, perceptual, and semantic drift across generations. To assess native asset usability beyond rendered appearance, Cyc3D further evaluates geometric structure, reference-image fidelity, mesh discretization and efficiency, and UV parameterization quality. Together, these diagnostics expose failures obscured by a single perceptual score and provide interpretable evidence of both model instability and representation defects. Experiments on five representative image-to-3D systems show that closed-source feed-forward models consistently outperform open-source optimization-based baselines in geometric fidelity, mesh quality, and cycle stability. Nevertheless, even the strongest methods achieve cycle-stability scores below 48, revealing a persistent gap between visually plausible generation and robust 3D object understanding.

</details>


### [35] [Attribute Token Arithmetic: Disentangled and Continuous Semantic Control for Visual Autoregressive Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28082)
*Xindi Yang,Yicheng Wu,Cheng Zhang,Jianfei Cai,Tien-Tsin Wong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出属性令牌算术（ATA）方法，通过在预训练的自回归视觉潜在空间中找到对应视觉属性（如衰老、肥胖、情绪）的语义方向，实现解耦且连续的属性控制。该方法仅需单张参考图像即可学习属性方向，无需模型重训练或大规模监督，生成时通过简单的算术运算实现属性的连续调整和组合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归文生图虽取得显著进展，但细粒度语义控制仍面临属性纠缠以及文本与细粒度视觉表示之间的不对齐问题。

Method: 受词嵌入中向量算术特性的启发，ATA在预训练自回归潜在空间中识别对应视觉属性的语义方向，从单张参考图像中学习这些方向（无需重训练），生成时通过与其他属性令牌的简单算术运算实现属性的连续调整与组合式叠加。

Result: 大量实验表明ATA实现了保持身份、细粒度和多属性的调整，在可控性、泛化性和计算效率上均优于现有自回归编辑基线。

Conclusion: ATA为自回归生成模型提供了一种无需重训练、仅需单参考图像的细粒度属性控制方案，在可控性、泛化性和效率方面优于现有方法，代码已开源。

Abstract: Autoregressive text-to-image generation has recently achieved remarkable progress, offering high-fidelity synthesis via a unified generative framework.
