However, fine-grained semantic control remains challenging due to the attribute entanglement and the misalignment between textual and fine-grained visual representations. In this paper, we introduce Attribute Token Arithmetic (ATA), a method that enables disentangled and continuous attribute control in visual autoregressive modelling. Inspired by the vector arithmetic property observed in word embeddings, ATA identifies semantic directions corresponding to visual attributes (e.g., aging, fatness, emotion) directly within the pretrained autoregressive latent space. These directions are learned from a single reference image, without model retraining or large-scale supervision. During generation, attributes can be continuously adjusted and compositionally combined through simple arithmetic operations with other attribute tokens. Extensive experiments demonstrate that ATA achieves identity-preserving, fine-grained, and multi-attribute adjustment, outperforming existing autoregressive editing baselines in controllability, generality, and computational efficiency. Our code will be available at https://github.com/Madaoer/ATA.

</details>


### [36] [Ex-Sim(3)-Reg: 2D-3D Correspondence Pruning via Extended Sim(3) Registration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28096)
*Pei An,Muyao Peng,Junfeng Ding,Jiaqi Yang,Liangliang Nan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Ex-Sim(3)-Reg算法，将图像到点云（I2P）配准中的2D-3D对应关系剪枝问题重新表述为扩展的Sim(3)配准问题，显式建模单目深度先验中的噪声，在多个基准数据集上实现了最高24.7%的配准召回率提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于学习的I2P配准方法在面临未见、低内点率或畸变等挑战性场景时，仍难以处理严重的外点问题。虽然将2D-3D对应提升到3D-3D对应并借助深度先验进行Sim(3)配准的方案有前景，但单目深度估计引入的噪声会破坏该方案的可靠性，因此需要显式建模深度噪声的鲁棒对应剪枝方法。

Method: 将对应剪枝问题重新表述为一个扩展的Sim(3)配准问题，以显式建模单目深度先验中不可忽略的深度噪声；并提出一个简单而有效的剪枝算法Ex-Sim(3)-Reg，同时提供理论分析来证明该方法的有效性。

Result: 在7-Scenes、RGBD-V2、ScanNet和TUM四个数据集上的大量实验表明，Ex-Sim(3)-Reg相比最先进的基线方法，配准召回率最高提升了24.7%。

Conclusion: 通过显式建模深度噪声并重构为扩展Sim(3)配准问题，Ex-Sim(3)-Reg提供了一种快速且鲁棒的2D-3D对应剪枝方案，在多个真实场景数据集上显著优于现有方法，且代码已开源。

Abstract: Learning-based image-to-point-cloud (I2P) registration has garnered increasing attention in recent years. Nevertheless, existing methods still struggle with severe outliers under challenging scenarios with unseen, low-inlier, or distorted cases. A fast and robust 2D-3D correspondence pruning method is therefore highly desirable. Recently, a promising scheme lifts 2D-3D correspondences to 3D-3D correspondences using depth priors, casting correspondence pruning as a Sim(3) registration problem. However, depth priors estimated from monocular images are inherently noisy, which undermines the reliability of this scheme. In this paper, to explicitly model non-negligible depth noise, we reformulate correspondence pruning as an extended Sim(3) registration problem and propose a simple yet effective pruning algorithm termed Ex-Sim(3)-Reg. We further provide a theoretical analysis to justify the effectiveness of our method. Extensive experiments on the 7-Scenes, RGBD-V2, ScanNet, and TUM datasets demonstrate that Ex-Sim(3)-Reg achieves up to \textbf{24.7\% improvement} in registration recall over state-of-the-art baseline methods. Code is released at github.com/anpei96/ex-sim3-demo

</details>


### [37] [Token-Budget Distillation: Transferring Full-Token Semantics to Compressed Video Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28138)
*Xiaoyang Guo,Guoping Luo,Jusheng Zhang,Keze Wang,Wenhao Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了Token-Budget Distillation (TBD)框架，通过参数高效微调+视觉token压缩+师生蒸馏，在固定token预算下保持视频VLM的性能，在R=10%压缩率下可保留97-100%的原始准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频VLM由于视频输入产生大量视觉token，导致微调和推理计算开销大。虽然视觉token压缩可以降低开销，但直接在压缩输入上适应模型会导致语义漂移和性能明显下降。

Method: TBD冻结预训练骨干，仅更新LoRA适配器，并集成FlashVID视觉token压缩。采用双路径师生设计：全token教师提供稳定监督，压缩学生通过任务损失、答案区域KL蒸馏、GT锚定边际蒸馏和可靠性感知KD控制进行优化。

Result: 在三个视频VLM骨干（LLaVA-Video、LLaVA-OneVision、Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct）和四个视频理解基准上评估。TBD在各种压缩率下均优于仅压缩的基线。在LLaVA-Video上R=10%时保留97.0%准确率，在LLaVA-OneVision上R=10%时达到100.0%相对准确率。

Conclusion: TBD使压缩学生模型能够在激进token压缩下恢复全token模型的语义行为，同时保持推理效率，验证了压缩+蒸馏相结合是视频VLM高效适配的有效途径。

Abstract: Adapting video vision-language models (VLMs) is computationally expensive because video inputs produce a large number of visual tokens, making both fine-tuning and inference costly. Although visual token compression can reduce this overhead, direct adaptation on compressed inputs often causes semantic drift and noticeable performance degradation. We present Token-Budget Distillation (TBD), a parameter-efficient fine-tuning framework for adapting video VLMs under a fixed token budget. TBD freezes the pretrained backbone, updates only LoRA adapters, and integrates FlashVID-based visual token compression into the video pathway. To preserve full-token semantics under compression, TBD employs a dual-path teacher-student design, where a full-token teacher provides stable supervision and a compressed student is optimized with task loss, answer-region KL distillation, GT-anchored margin distillation, and reliability-aware KD control. This design enables the student to recover the semantic behavior of the full-token model while remaining efficient under aggressive token reduction. We evaluate TBD on three video VLM backbones, including LLaVA-Video, LLaVA-OneVision, and Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct, across four video understanding benchmarks. TBD consistently outperforms compression-only baselines under both moderate and aggressive compression. On LLaVA-Video at retention ratio R = 10 percent, TBD preserves 97.0 percent of the Vanilla model's average accuracy; on LLaVA-OneVision at R = 10 percent, it achieves an average score of 58.4 and matches 100.0 percent relative accuracy.

</details>


### [38] [Dual-Stream Semantic Guidance with Prototype Anchor Calibration for Source-Fully-Free Adaptation of Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28145)
*Weiwei Xiang,Shun Peng,Guangyi Xiao,Hao Chen,Lei Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出DSSG及扩展DSSG-PAC框架，解决Source-Free域适应中视觉-语言模型面临的双重语义漂移问题。通过双流语义引导模块（DSG）融合领域特定知识与全局类别锚定，结合动态跨模态知识蒸馏（CMKD），并在DSSG-PAC中引入原型锚定校准以降低计算开销，实现SOTA性能且减少18.9%的适应时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Source-Fully-Free域适应在无源数据和无源模型条件下适配视觉-语言模型时存在双重语义漂移：静态漂移源于固定类嵌入的刚性，动态漂移源于生成标题的偏差，导致严重语义错配并加剧稳定-可塑性困境。

Method: 提出端到端框架DSSG，核心包括：(1) 双流语义引导（DSG）模块，整合标题流提供领域特定知识与类锚流维持全局类别一致性；(2) 动态跨模态知识蒸馏（CMKD）模块，利用演化的教师分布校准师生一致性；(3) 进一步提出原型锚定校准（PAC），周期性地校准原型锚并缓存至下次校准，减少文本侧冗余计算。同时建立SFF-DA风险界，关联学生风险与语义教师质量及师生差异。

Result: 大量实验表明DSSG在多个基准上一致超越现有SOTA方法；DSSG-PAC在保持适应性能的同时，总适应时间降低18.9%。

Conclusion: DSSG框架通过双流语义引导有效调和了细粒度可塑性与全局稳定性，解决了SFF-DA中的双重语义漂移问题；DSSG-PAC在计算效率与适应性能之间取得了良好平衡。

Abstract: Source-Fully-Free Domain Adaptation (SFF-DA) has emerged as a strategic paradigm to adapt Vision-Language Models (VLMs) without any access to source data or task-specific source models. However, we identify a critical Dual Semantic Drift that hinders this process: static drift arising from the rigidity of fixed class embeddings, and dynamic drift stemming from the divergence of generated captions, causing severe semantic misalignment that intensifies the stability-plasticity dilemma. To address this, we propose DSSG (Dual-Stream Semantic Guidance), an end-to-end framework that reconciles fine-grained plasticity with global stability. Our core contribution is the Dual Semantic Guidance (DSG) module, which integrates a caption stream for domain-specific knowledge with a class-anchor stream to anchor global categorical consistency. Furthermore, a Dynamic Cross-Modal Knowledge Distillation (CMKD) module is introduced to leverage the evolving teacher distribution for calibrating teacher-student consistency. Building upon DSSG, we further introduce Prototype Anchor Calibration (PAC), yielding DSSG-PAC, which periodically calibrates prototype anchors and caches them until the next calibration. This design reduces redundant text-side computation while preserving the adaptability of class guidance to the evolving text space. We further establish SFF-DA risk bounds that relate student risk to semantic-teacher quality and teacher--student discrepancy. Extensive experiments demonstrate that DSSG consistently outperforms current state-of-the-art methods across multiple benchmarks, while DSSG-PAC largely preserves its adaptation performance with 18.9% lower total adaptation time. The code is available at https://github.com/mrmenand/DSSG.

</details>


### [39] [Empowering Local Agriculture: A Deep Learning-Powered Web System for Identifying Bangladeshi Mango Varieties](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28161)
*Monowar Islam,Safaruzzaman Shovo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于深度学习的孟加拉芒果品种自动识别Web系统，使用三种预训练CNN模型（ResNet18、ResNet50、EfficientNetB0）在2,013张高质量芒果图像上进行微调，其中EfficientNetB0表现最佳（测试准确率97.36%），并集成到Streamlit应用中供用户上传图片获取品种预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 孟加拉芒果品种识别面临挑战：相近品种外观相似，且真实场景下拍摄的图像条件多变，传统目视识别方法不可靠，亟需自动化的品种识别工具。

Method: 收集2,013张3024x4032像素的芒果图像，划分为9个类别（Bari-4和Bari-7合并为Bari类），按70/15/15比例划分训练/验证/测试集并应用数据增强；对ResNet18、ResNet50和EfficientNetB0三个预训练CNN模型在一致训练设置下进行微调，将最优模型集成到Streamlit Web应用中。

Result: EfficientNetB0表现最佳，验证准确率98.01%，测试准确率97.36%，远超ResNet18（86.47%）和ResNet50（78.55%）；各类别F1分数在0.93-0.99之间，Bari类F1为0.97；模型约400万参数，适合轻量级部署。

Conclusion: 所提出的EfficientNetB0模型结合Streamlit Web应用为孟加拉芒果品种识别提供了准确、便捷且实用的工具，展示了深度学习在孟加拉农业应用中的潜力。

Abstract: Mango variety identification in Bangladesh is challenging because closely related cultivars can have similar visual characteristics and images are often captured under varying real-world conditions. This work presents a deep learning-based web system for automatic identification of Bangladeshi mango varieties. We collected 2,013 high-quality mango images (3024x4032 pixels) from local markets and farms and organized them into nine classes, combining Bari-4 and Bari-7 as a single Bari class. The dataset was divided into training (70%), validation (15%), and test (15%) sets, with image augmentation applied to improve model generalization. Three pretrained CNN architectures, ResNet18, ResNet50, and EfficientNetB0, were fine-tuned under consistent training settings. EfficientNetB0 achieved the best performance, obtaining 98.01% validation accuracy and 97.36% test accuracy, compared with 86.47% and 78.55% test accuracy for ResNet18 and ResNet50, respectively. Class-wise F1-scores for EfficientNetB0 ranged from 0.93 to 0.99, while the Bari class achieved an F1-score of 0.97. The selected EfficientNetB0 model has approximately 4 million parameters, making it suitable for lightweight deployment. We integrated the model into a Streamlit web application that enables users to upload a mango image and receive a predicted variety with class probabilities. The system provides an accessible, practical tool for mango identification and demonstrates the potential of deep learning for supporting agricultural applications in Bangladesh.

</details>


### [40] [Manifold4D: Denoising on Point Cloud Rendered Manifolds for Video Re-shooting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28174)
*Yongqi Mao,Zijia Dai,Zhishuo Liu,Wei Xu,Kaiwei Wang,Guotao Meng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MANIFOLD4D，将渲染结果直接注入流匹配模型的初始噪声中，而非作为显式条件输入，使几何信息内嵌于噪声流形，源视频成为唯一视觉条件，从而解决视频重拍摄中渲染与源视频竞争导致的信任困境。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频重拍摄方法将渲染结果和源视频同时作为视觉条件输入网络，二者在每步去噪中竞争，模型面临"该相信多少渲染结果"的信任困境，导致在分布外数据上轨迹控制或视觉质量下降。

Method: 将像素对齐的渲染结果直接注入流匹配（flow matching）的初始噪声中，使生成从携带几何信息的新噪声流形出发而非标准高斯噪声，渲染仅被使用一次，网络不再需要学习如何读取渲染结果，源视频成为唯一的视觉条件。

Result: 在DAVIS-Traj基准和Vista4D评估集上，MANIFOLD4D在所有指标上达到最佳相机控制精度，旋转误差较最强基线降低25%（Vista4D为27%），平移误差最高降低32%，视频保真度与之持平，真实世界新视角光度质量领先；用户研究中在轨迹跟随和动态一致性上优势明显。

Conclusion: 随着偏航幅度超出训练范围，方法优势进一步扩大；即使在渲染被故意破坏时，模型仍能从源视频恢复正确的动态运动，证明几何先验引导生成但不会覆盖生成，有效解决了渲染与源视频之间的信任困境。

Abstract: Video re-shooting re-renders a monocular video of a dynamic scene along a user-specified camera trajectory, and the dominant recipe supplies the target geometry explicitly: per-frame depth lifts the source video into a 4D point cloud, which is rasterized along the trajectory into a point cloud render. Because the render and the source video are both handed to the network as visual conditions, they compete at every denoising step, leaving the model with a trust dilemma --- how much of the render to believe --- which can degrade trajectory control or visual quality on data outside the training distribution. We argue that a render already pixel-aligned with the target view does not need to be supplied as an explicit conditioning stream at all. We propose MANIFOLD4D, which injects the render directly into the initial noise of flow matching, so that generation no longer departs from standard Gaussian noise but from a new noise manifold carrying geometric information, leaving the source video as the only visual condition. The render is thus used exactly once, and the network is never asked to learn how to read it; in subsequent denoising steps the model can focus on the source video. On our DAVIS-Traj benchmark and on the Vista4D evaluation set, MANIFOLD4D attains the best camera-control accuracy on every metric, lowering rotation error by 25% and 27% and translation error by up to 32% over the strongest baseline, while matching it in video fidelity and leading on real-world novel-view photometric quality. In a user study, our method achieves clear advantages in trajectory following and dynamic consistency. The gap widens as the yaw amplitude grows past the training range, and the model still recovers correct dynamic motion from the source video when the render is deliberately corrupted, confirming that the geometric prior guides generation without overriding it.

</details>


### [41] [EXPOSE: Explainable and Domain-Robust Embeddings from Pathology Vision Foundation Models using Sparse Autoencoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28191)
*Anja Witte,Maximilian Lennartz,Jan Baumbach,Guido Sauter,Stefan Bonn,Patrick Fuhlert,Marina Zimmermann*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出EXPOSE框架，利用稀疏自编码器（SAEs）作为可解释瓶颈层，识别并抑制视觉基础模型（VFMs）嵌入中的领域特定成分，从而提升计算病理学中的跨域泛化能力，在前列腺癌数据集上验证了该方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉基础模型在计算病理学中被广泛使用，但对染色差异、组织制备和扫描硬件等域迁移敏感。其根本原因是VFM嵌入将生物学信息与领域特定信息纠缠在一起，阻碍了跨域泛化。

Method: EXPOSE框架包含三个关键步骤：(1) 训练VFM特征的稀疏表示（SAEs作为可解释瓶颈）；(2) 使用线性分类器识别领域特定的潜在维度；(3) 在下游复发预测前屏蔽这些领域特定特征，且无需重新训练骨干模型。

Result: 在包含多个采集域的大型前列腺癌数据集上的实验表明：SAE特征能同时捕获领域特定和任务特定信息，且这些信息在潜在空间中部分解耦。移除领域特定特征后，跨域性能得到提升，嵌入鲁棒性也通过Domain Robustness Index (DoRI)得到了验证。

Conclusion: EXPOSE通过SAEs实现可解释的跨域嵌入去偏，在不重新训练骨干模型的前提下有效提升了VFM在病理学跨域场景中的泛化能力和鲁棒性，代码已开源。

Abstract: Vision Foundation Models (VFMs) are widely used in computational pathology but remain sensitive to domain shifts arising from variations in staining, tissue preparation, and scanner hardware. A key limitation is that VFM embeddings entangle biological with domain-specific information, hindering cross-domain generalization. We propose Explainable Probing of Cross-Domain Sparse Embeddings (EXPOSE), a framework that uses Sparse Autoencoders (SAEs) as an explainable bottleneck to identify and suppress domain-specific components in VFM embeddings. We train a sparse representation of VFM features, use a linear classifier to identify domain-specific latent dimensions, and mask these features prior to downstream relapse prediction without retraining the backbone model. Experiments on a large prostate cancer dataset with multiple acquisition domains show that SAE features capture both domain- and task-specific information, which are partially disentangled in the latent space. Removing domain-specific features improves cross-domain performance and increases embedding robustness as measured by the Domain Robustness Index (DoRI). Code is available at https://github.com/imsb-uke/expose .

</details>


### [42] [Locate Anything in Videos: Rethinking Efficient Generative Spatio-Temporal Video Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28192)
*Hanoona Rasheed,Haania Siddiqui,Ming-Hsuan Yang,Fahad Shahbaz Khan,Salman Khan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出并行管状解码（PTD）新框架，将时空视频定位分解为时间块和多个时间条件空间块并行解码，消除token级和轨迹级依赖，将顺序解码深度压缩为固定2轮，相比自回归解码将管延迟降低79倍、空间解码吞吐量提升92倍，同时提高定位精度并支持零样本泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态大语言模型在时空视频定位任务中采用自回归方式逐一生成密集定位轨迹，存在两个核心问题：解码延迟随管长度线性增长，以及定位误差在时间上逐帧传播累积。这些问题严重制约了模型的实时性与精度。

Method: 提出并行管状解码（PTD）框架：1）将时空定位分解为一个时间块和多个时间条件空间块，各空间块可同时解码；2）引入解耦块注意力（Decoupled Block Attention），在保留视频-查询共享上下文的同时消除跨框依赖；3）设计定位感知策略优化，用于时间边界和空间几何的联合优化。

Result: 在VidSTG数据集上，PTD相比标准自回归解码将管完成延迟降低79倍、空间解码吞吐量提升92倍，同时提升了定位精度。基于4B骨干的紧凑模型在VidSTG和HC-STVG上均表现良好，并能零样本泛化到时间定位、接地视频问答和指代视频目标跟踪等下游任务。

Conclusion: 并行管状生成是视频自回归定位的高效且有效的替代方案，以固定解码深度实现高吞吐量和低延迟，同时保持甚至提升定位精度，证明了并行解码范式在多模态视频理解任务中的巨大潜力。

Abstract: Spatio-temporal video grounding (STVG) requires models to identify when a referred event occurs and localize the target entity throughout that interval. Existing multimodal large language models typically serialize dense localization trajectories autoregressively, causing decoding latency to grow with tube length and allowing localization errors to propagate across time. We introduce Parallel Tube Decoding (PTD), a generative formulation that decomposes grounding into a temporal block followed by time-conditioned spatial blocks decoded simultaneously. This removes both token-level and trajectory-level dependencies, reducing the sequential decoding depth to a fixed $1 + 1$ rounds, independent of tube length. To enable parallel spatial generation, we introduce Decoupled Block Attention, which preserves access to shared video-query context while eliminating cross-box dependencies, together with localization-aware policy optimization for temporal boundaries and spatial geometry. On VidSTG, PTD reduces Tube Completion Latency by 79x and increases spatial decoding throughput by 92x over standard autoregressive decoding, while also improving grounding accuracy. With a compact 4B backbone, our model performs favorably well on VidSTG and HC-STVG, and generalizes zero-shot to temporal grounding, grounded VideoQA, and referring video object tracking. Our results show parallel tube generation is an efficient and effective alternative to autoregressive localization in videos.

</details>


### [43] [UniLipi: A Unified Multi-Script OCR for Historical Indic Manuscripts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28195)
*Tathagata Ghosh,Sai Madhusudan Gunda,Simran Singh Sandral,Ravi Kiran Sarvadevabhatla*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出UniLipi，一个统一的多脚本手写印度文本OCR模型，在13种印度文字上联合训练，能够处理真实手稿中的几何变形、行长度变化和污损干扰。通过脚本感知的合成数据生成支持超低资源场景，并可作为基础预训练模型迁移到当代手写和非印度文字（如藏文、意大利文、拉丁文、中文）上，同时预测脚本身份和字符数量以支持编目工作流。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有手写印度文本OCR方法通常针对单一文字开发，需要大量脚本特定定制，难以在不同收藏集之间规模化部署，缺乏统一的多脚本解决方案。

Method: UniLipi在单一框架内联合训练13种印度文字，直接处理行几何极端变化、行长差异和孔洞/污渍/插图等非文本干扰；采用脚本感知的合成手稿数据生成来应对超低资源条件，并同时预测转录、脚本身份和每行字符数量。

Result: UniLipi在历史手稿多脚本OCR中表现良好，其学习表征可作为基础预训练模型，在当代印度手写和非印度文字（藏文、意大利文、拉丁文、中文）上均获得良好OCR性能，同时支持脚本识别和字符计数预测。

Conclusion: UniLipi展示了统一多脚本OCR范式在印度文字历史上的可行性和有效性，在超低资源条件下通过合成数据降低对真实标注的依赖，并验证了跨脚本迁移学习的能力，为大规模手稿数字化和编目提供了实用的通用解决方案。

Abstract: Optical character recognition (OCR) for handwritten Indic manuscripts is essential for large-scale digitization and computational access to manuscript heritage. However, existing approaches are typically developed for one script at a time and require substantial script-specific customization. This limits scalability and practical deployment across diverse collections. We present UniLipi, a unified multi-script OCR model for handwritten Indic manuscripts trained jointly across 13 Indic scripts within a single framework. UniLipi directly handles realistic manuscript conditions, including extreme variation in line geometry, large variation in line length, and partial interruptions caused by non-textual manuscript entities such as holes, stains, or pictorial illustrations. To operate effectively under ultra low-resource conditions, the model leverages script-aware synthetic manuscript data generation, substantially reducing reliance on large volumes of real annotated data. Beyond historical manuscripts, we show that UniLipi serves as an effective foundational pretrained model. Specifically, its learned representations enable good OCR performance for contemporary Indic handwriting and extend to several non-Indic scripts, including Tibetan, Italian, Latin, and Chinese scripts. In addition to transcription, UniLipi predicts script identity and per-line native character counts, supporting practical manuscript cataloging workflows.

</details>


### [44] [Cut-ViT: Task-Specific Model Pruning via Gram Anchoring Subspace Consistency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28205)
*Jianjian Yin,Liulei Li,Tao Chen,Yi Chen,Yazhou Yao,Wenguan Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Cut-ViT，一种面向任务的视觉基础模型剪枝流水线。通过构造空间和语义维度的gram锚定矩阵并进行子空间分解，结合基无关和残差约束实现剪枝前后子空间对齐，并引入谱熵自适应机制来量化特征流形的信息密度，从而将剪枝目标适配到特定下游任务。实验表明Cut-ViT在单个A100 GPU上约一分钟即可获得不同稀疏度的子网络，仅使用前人方法20.9%的时间和45.5%的GPU内存，在六个任务九个数据集上达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉基础模型剪枝方法专注于在单一数据集上进行刚性的点对点token对齐，存在两个局限性：一是鲁棒性退化，二是任务特化能力不足。作者旨在克服这两个问题，提出一种既能保持模型鲁棒性又能适配特定下游任务的剪枝方案。

Method: Cut-ViT包含三个关键组件：(1) 从空间和语义两个视角构造gram锚定矩阵，并通过子空间分解提取对应子空间基；(2) 采用基无关和残差约束，沿空间和通道维度对齐原生与剪枝后DINOv3模型的gram子空间，使子网络继承原生模型的鲁棒特征表示；(3) 设计谱熵自适应机制，量化特征流形沿空间和通道维度的信息密度，从而将剪枝目标自适应地调整到特定下游任务。

Result: Cut-ViT在单个A100 GPU上约一分钟即可生成不同稀疏度的子网络，相比先前方法仅需20.9%的时间和45.5%的GPU内存，在六个下游任务九个数据集上均取得最先进（SOTA）的性能。

Conclusion: Cut-ViT通过子空间对齐结合谱熵自适应，有效解决了现有剪枝方法鲁棒性退化与任务特化不足的问题，实现了高效、稳健且任务适配的视觉基础模型剪枝。

Abstract: Pruning visual foundation models has attracted considerable attention. However, existing methods focus on rigid point-to-point token alignment on a single dataset for pruning, suffering from two limitations: i) robustness degradation, and ii) task-specificity deficiency. To address these limitations, we propose a task-specific pruning pipeline, named Cut-ViT. Specifically, we first construct gram anchoring matrices from both spatial and semantic perspectives, and perform the subspace decomposition to extract the corresponding subspace bases. Basis-agnostic and residual constraints are then adopted to align the gram subspaces between the native and pruned DINOv3 models along spatial and channel dimensions, enabling subnetworks to inherit robust feature representations of native DINOv3. Furthermore, we design spectral entropy adaptation, which quantifies the information density of feature manifolds along spatial and channel dimensions, thereby adapting the pruning objective to specific downstream tasks. Experiments show that Cut-ViT requires approximately one minute on a single A100 GPU to obtain subnetworks at various sparsity levels, using only 20.9% of the time and 45.5% of the GPU memory compared with previous methods, while achieving SOTA performance on six tasks across nine datasets.

</details>


### [45] [NumBench: Diagnosing Counting Failures in Text-to-Image Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28206)
*Sandeep Wadhwa,Mayank Vatsa,Richa Singh,Parrva Chirag Shah,Prakhar Galriya*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出NumBench，一个包含64万提示词、横跨1600个类别和1至100个计数的文本到图像（T2I）计数基准，并开发了一个实例竞争有限可解析图像区域的流程模型，预测了低占用率下近二次方的碰撞赤字。同时提出置信度加权数值精确度评分（CWNPS）用于可扩展评估，实验表明所有方法在50个以上物体时性能显著下降，计数范围是影响最大的因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的T2I模型在生成物体数量时经常出错，但已有基准要么规模太小，要么控制不够严谨，无法解释失败的根本原因。需要一个大规模、严格受控的基准来系统性地研究计数失败的原因和影响因素。

Method: 构建NumBench基准（64万提示词，1-100计数范围，涵盖物体组成、空间引导、外观条件等因素的因子设计），提出流程模型解释计数缺陷，开发置信度加权数值精确度评分（CWNPS）作为评估指标，并进行14,400图像的人工研究验证自动化评估。

Result: 所有评估方法在计数超过50时性能显著下降；计数范围是影响最大的因素，其次是布局和组成；网格引导在各类引导布局中最强，与协调放置预测一致；人工研究支持计数至50的自动化评估可靠性；243个自然语言提示结果显示可迁移性。

Conclusion: NumBench为T2I计数能力提供了大规模系统化评估框架，揭示了计数随请求数量急剧下降的规律，以及布局引导对计数精度的积极作用，为改进T2I模型的计数能力提供了方向。

Abstract: Text-to-image (T2I) models often generate the wrong number of objects, yet existing benchmarks are too small or weakly controlled to explain why. We introduce \textbf{NumBench}, a benchmark of 640{,}000 prompts spanning 1{,}600 categories and counts from 1 to 100. Its factorial design varies object composition, spatial guidance, and appearance conditions while balancing counts and category exposure. We also develop a process model in which requested instances compete for a finite set of resolvable image regions. The model predicts a near-quadratic collision deficit at low occupancy and shows how coordinated placement reduces it. For scalable evaluation, we propose the Confidence-Weighted Numeric Precision Score (\cwnps), which aggregates three calibrated detectors and discounts uncertain proposals. Across five commercial systems, two open models, and two specialized counting methods, performance declines sharply with requested count; all evaluated methods are weak above 50 objects. Count range has the largest measured effect, followed by layout and composition. Grid guidance is strongest among guided layouts, consistent with the coordination prediction, although the analysis does not establish collision as the sole cause. A 14{,}400-image human study supports automated evaluation through count 50, while results on 243 natural-language prompts show transfer beyond NumBench templates.

</details>


### [46] [Explainable Diabetic Retinopathy Classification Using Vision Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28207)
*Abhishek Verma,Anila Krishna,Abhishek Gajanan Bankar,Juan Miguel Lopez Alcaraz*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究探索了基于视觉基础模型（DINOv2、CLIP、ViT）和多种迁移学习策略（全量微调、线性探测、LoRA）的可解释糖尿病视网膜病变分类框架，在ODIR和APTOS数据集上验证性能，并结合Grad-CAM和HiResCAM等可解释性方法评估模型注意力是否与临床病变区域一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 糖尿病视网膜病变是可预防性失明的主要原因，需要准确且可信赖的自动化筛查系统。现有模型虽性能优异但缺乏可解释性，难以获得临床信任，因此需要同时兼顾预测性能与可解释性的分类框架。

Method: 使用三种视觉基础模型（DINOv2、CLIP、ViT）作为骨干网络，结合三种迁移学习策略（全量微调、线性探测、LoRA）构建分类框架；在ODIR数据集上内部训练评估，在APTOS数据集上外部评估泛化能力；通过等渗回归进行校准分析；使用Grad-CAM和HiResCAM生成解释图，并在IDRiD数据集上基于专家标注的病变掩膜用Dice、IoU和Pointing Game指标进行定量评估。

Result: DINOv2-LoRA在内部评估中取得最高AUROC（0.758），DINOv2全量微调和ViT全量微调在外部评估中取得最高AUROC（0.920）。LoRA作为参数高效方案可媲美全量微调，基础模型尤其是DINOv2展现出强大预测能力，解释图定量评估支持模型注意力与临床相关视网膜病变的对应关系。

Conclusion: 视觉基础模型特别是DINOv2能为DR分类提供强预测性能，LoRA是高效的参数替代方案；量化评估解释图有助于验证模型注意力是否聚焦于临床相关的视网膜病变区域，为可信任的自动化筛查提供支持。

Abstract: Diabetic retinopathy (DR) is a major cause of preventable blindness, creating a need for accurate and trustworthy automated screening. This study investigates an explainable DR classification framework using vision foundation models and multiple transfer learning strategies. Three backbones, DINOv2, CLIP, and Vision Transformer (ViT), were evaluated using full fine-tuning, linear probing, and Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA). Models were trained and internally evaluated on the ODIR dataset and externally evaluated on APTOS to assess generalization. DINOv2-LoRA achieved the highest internal AUROC of 0.758, while DINOv2 full fine-tuning and ViT full fine-tuning achieved the highest external AUROC of 0.920. Calibration was further assessed using reliability analysis after isotonic regression. For explainability, Grad-CAM and HiResCAM were evaluated against expert-annotated lesion masks from the IDRiD dataset using Dice, Intersection over Union (IoU), and Pointing Game metrics. The results demonstrate that foundation models, particularly DINOv2, can provide strong predictive performance, while LoRA offers a parameter-efficient alternative to full fine-tuning. Quantitative evaluation of explanation maps further supports the assessment of whether model attention corresponds to clinically relevant retinal lesions.

</details>


### [47] [WALDO: One-Shot Exemplar-Conditioned Object Detection in Cluttered Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28216)
*Kishor Datta Gupta,Ahmed Rafi Hasan,Md. Mahfuzur Rahman,Md. Sadman Haque,Mohd Ariful Haque*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出WALDO，一个仅3.4M可训练参数的one-shot目标检测头，利用冻结的V-JEPA 2.1世界模型特征进行实例定位和存在性判断，无需在主干网络上计算梯度。通过合成训练片段和负样本控制来关闭捷径，在35个杂乱场景上实现了0.461的catalogue AP@50，优于Grounding DINO baseline（0.306），但与DINOv3对比实验证明预训练目标而非输入分辨率是性能提升的关键。然而实例级Success@1仅为0.190，与类别水平的随机基线持平，表明世界模型特征可迁移至定位精度和缺失检测，但不能迁移至实例身份识别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究大规模视觉语言模型（LVLMs）所具备的one-shot实例定位能力是否可以从更便宜的世界模型预训练表示中获取，即验证世界模型自监督预训练目标本身是否蕴含了足够的视觉定位与无关实例判别能力，从而避免依赖昂贵的大模型。

Method: 1) 提出WALDO检测头：3.4M可训练参数，读取冻结的V-JEPA 2.1特征，联合预测目标定位与存在性；2) 设计实例级训练数据合成策略：从标注框挖掘exemplars并构造排除参照实例但保留同类干扰物的缺失样本；3) 识别并修复关键捷径：裁剪尺寸泄露标签问题，通过负向对照关闭该捷径；4) 与DINOv3进行匹配576-token网格的实验以隔离预训练目标的影响。

Result: 在35个held-out杂乱场景上，WALDO达到0.461的catalogue AP@50，优于Grounding DINO baseline的0.306。将V-JEPA替换为DINOv3后，within-category缺失检测AUROC从0.880降至0.726，实例AP@50从0.201降至0.141，证明预训练目标而非输入分辨率是提升来源。但实例级Success@1仅0.190，与0.190的类别随机水平持平，说明实例身份区分能力不足。此外，论文报告了朴素实现中裁剪尺寸可作为捷径使absence AUROC达到0.9998而不参考exemplar的负面结果。

Conclusion: 世界模型特征（V-JEPA 2.1）能够以极低成本迁移到one-shot目标定位和存在性检测任务，在此方面甚至优于prompted Grounding DINO。但世界模型表示缺乏实例级身份判别力，无法实现真正的实例识别，后续工作需探索如何增强实例区分能力或结合其他信号。

Abstract: Locating a specific object instance in a cluttered scene using a single reference image and a short description, and reporting when that instance is absent, large vision-language models usually address this task. We ask whether the same capability is available far more cheaply, from representations already learned by a world-model pretraining objective. We present WALDO, a one-shot exemplar- and language-conditioned detection head with 3.4M trainable parameters that reads frozen V-JEPA 2.1 features to jointly predict object localization and target presence, with no gradient on the backbone. Because exemplar-conditioned supervision is scarce, we synthesize training episodes from instance annotations, mining exemplars from ground-truth boxes and constructing absence cases that exclude the referenced instance while leaving same-category distractors in view. This is easy to get wrong: in the obvious implementation, crop size alone predicts the label, and a head trained on it reaches 0.9998 absence AUROC without ever consulting the exemplar, and we report the negative controls that close the shortcut. On 35 held-out cluttered scenes, WALDO achieves a 0.461 catalogue AP@50, compared to 0.306 for a prompted Grounding DINO baseline under an identical scorer. Substituting DINOv3 for V-JEPA under a matched 576-token grid drops within-category absence AUROC from 0.880 to 0.726 and instance AP@50 from 0.201 to 0.141, isolating the pretraining objective rather than input resolution as the source of the gain. Instance-level Success@1, however, reaches only 0.190 against a 0.190 category-chance floor: world-model features transfer to localization precision and absence detection but not to instance identity.

</details>


### [48] [Focus Where It Counts: A Salience-Driven Vision-Language Model for Low Vision Assistance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28218)
*Jiazhao Liang,Hao Huang,Shuaihang Yuan,Congcong Wen,Geeta Chandra Raju Bethala,Giles Hamilton-Fletcher,Yu Hao,John-Ross Rizzo,Mengyu Wang,Anthony Tzes,Yi Fang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于显著性的图像描述框架(Salience-LLaVA)，通过构建三个显著性感知数据集(Salience COCO、Salience Flickr、Salience VizWiz)，使视觉语言模型能够按照场景元素对人类辅助的重要性排序生成描述，并部署在辅助眼镜上验证其实际应用价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的视觉语言模型主要面向通用图像描述，未显式建模人类感知优先级，导致在面向盲人或低视力人群的辅助技术中无法突出场景中最相关的信息。因此需要一种能够按重要性排序描述场景元素的模型。

Method: 首先构建三个带有物体级显著性标注的数据集，标注反映了低视力用户在不同环境中最相关的视觉信息，并由低视力参与者验证；在此基础上提出Salience-LLaVA模型，引入显著性线索生成按重要性顺序描述物体的字幕；同时引入SCMI指标评估排序准确性；最后部署在辅助眼镜上进行实际验证。

Result: 本文构建了三个经低视力参与者验证的显著性感知数据集，提出了Salience-LLaVA模型，引入SCMI评估指标以衡量描述中物体排序的准确性，并在辅助眼镜上完成部署演示了实际可用性。

Conclusion: 通过将人类感知优先级融入视觉语言模型，本文提出的方法能够为低视力用户提供更符合其信息需求的辅助描述，展示了显著性驱动的描述框架在辅助技术中的实际应用潜力。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) are rapidly progressing and offer promising capabilities for assistive technologies supporting persons with blindness or low vision. However, existing VLMs are primarily designed for general-purpose captioning and do not explicitly model human perceptual priorities, thereby limiting their ability to emphasize the most relevant information in a scene. To address this gap, we propose a salience-driven captioning framework that prioritizes scene elements according to their importance for human-centered assistance. We curate three salience-aware datasets, namely, Salience COCO, Salience Flickr, and Salience VizWiz, with object-level salience annotations designed to reflect the visual information most relevant to low vision users across different environments. Building on these datasets, we introduce Salience-LLaVA, a salience-aware VLM that incorporates salience cues to generate captions in which important elements are mentioned in the order of importance. Our work makes four main contributions. We build salience-aware datasets verified by low vision participants, propose Salience-LLaVA to describe objects in the order of importance, introduce SCMI to evaluate ordering accuracy, and deploy the system on assistive glasses to demonstrate real-world practicality. Code and datasets are available at: https://github.com/topo-focus/Topofocus

</details>


### [49] [RASA: Disentangled Spatial-Motional Priors for Cross-Identity Character Animation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28219)
*Zhen Xiao,Zhen Shen,Zhaofan Qiu,Ting Yao,Xueliang Liu,Tao Mei*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出RASA框架，通过将结构化先验注入Diffusion Transformer，解耦跨身份角色动画中的空间映射与运动控制，在运动保真度和视觉质量上显著超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨身份角色动画的核心挑战在于空间映射（位置、尺度、骨骼比例对齐）与运动控制（关节细化、体积一致性、视角连贯性）两种能力的天然纠缠，现有方法难以同时处理好这两方面。

Method: RASA包含两阶段：1）空间先验校准器（SPC）融合参考身份与驱动姿态，生成空间锚定的初始噪声潜变量；2）内在运动引导器（IMG）将形状无关的SMPL关节参数编码为语义运动向量，注入DiT中间层以补充基础姿态条件。同时构建了CIM-Bench高质量基准用于评估。

Result: 在自建的CIM-Bench基准上，RASA在运动保真度和视觉质量两方面均显著优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: 解耦的空间先验与运动先验是实现稳健角色动画的关键，该工作为该领域建立了新的研究范式。

Abstract: Cross-identity character animation aims to drive a target identity from a reference image to follow the motion of a source character from a driving video. The core challenge lies in the inherent entanglement of two capabilities: cross-identity spatial mapping (aligning position, scale, and skeletal proportions) and motion control (refining joint articulation, volumetric consistency, and view coherence). We introduce Reference-Aware Structural Alignment (RASA), a framework that disentangles spatial mapping from motion control by injecting structured priors into a Diffusion Transformer (DiT). Our approach has two stages. First, a Spatial Prior Calibrator (SPC) fuses reference identity with driving pose to generate a spatially grounded initial noise latent, ensuring correct positioning, scaling, and alignment with the driving skeleton. Second, an Inherent Motional Guider (IMG) encodes shape-agnostic SMPL articulation parameters into a semantic motion vector beyond appearance-biased 2D keypoints. Injected into intermediate DiT layers, this vector complements the base pose condition for anatomically consistent articulation and view-aware volumetric refinement. We curate CIM-Bench, a high-quality benchmark with rigorous curation, for evaluation. Extensive experiments show RASA significantly outperforms state-of-the-art methods in motion fidelity and visual quality. Our work establishes a new paradigm showing disentangled spatial and motional priors are key to robust character animation. Project page: https://hidream.ai.github.io/RASA/

</details>


### [50] [WilLaGS: Latent-Conditional 3D Appearance Fields for Robust Gaussian Splatting In-the-Wild](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28240)
*Yuhao Bai,Qianqiu Tan,Lilong Chen,Huanhuan Lv,Lijun Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: WilLaGS是一个统一的3D场景重建与生成式外观合成框架，通过β-VAE学习连续全局外观流形，结合3D神经外观场生成动态Tri-Plane特征以编码空间变化的局部光照，并利用Teacher-Student自监督感知掩码机制抑制瞬态伪影，在多个数据集上实现了最先进的渲染质量和实时效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3DGS方法在处理无约束野外场景时面临两大挑战：一是独立且离散的外观嵌入难以捕捉连续的环境变化；二是无法有效建模空间变化的局部光照。这些局限性严重影响了室外场景重建的外观一致性和渲染质量。

Method: 提出WilLaGS统一框架，包含三个核心组件：(1) 生成式外观模型，使用β-VAE学习结构化且连续的全局外观流形；(2) 基于条件潜码构建的3D神经外观场，生成动态Tri-Plane特征来编码空间变化的局部光照效应；(3) 基于Teacher-Student(EMA)架构的自监督感知掩码机制，通过感知差异识别不一致区域并抑制瞬态伪影。

Result: 在多个数据集上的大量实验表明，WilLaGS在重建质量和新颖视图外观合成方面均达到最先进水平，同时保持了3DGS的实时渲染效率。

Conclusion: WilLaGS通过引入生成式连续外观建模和自监督瞬态抑制机制，有效解决了无约束野外场景中外观连续变化和瞬态物体干扰的难题，为3DGS在复杂真实场景中的应用提供了统一且高效的解决方案。

Abstract: 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) delivers real-time and high-fidelity rendering but remains challenged by unconstrained in-the-wild scenes, where drastic appearance variations and transient objects violate multi-view consistency. Existing methods are fundamentally limited by independent and discrete embeddings that struggle to capture continuous environmental changes or model spatially-varying local illumination. To address these limitations, we propose \textbf{WilLaGS}, a unified framework for robust 3D scene reconstruction and generative appearance synthesis under unconstrained settings. Specifically, we introduce a generative appearance model where a $β$-VAE learns a structured and continuous manifold of global appearance. Conditioned on the latent code, we construct a 3D neural appearance field that generates dynamic Tri-Plane features to encode spatially-varying local illumination effects. Furthermore, to suppress transient artifacts, we present a self-supervised perceptual masking mechanism that leverages a Teacher-Student (EMA) architecture to derive a stable scene consensus, robustly identifying inconsistent regions via perceptual discrepancies. Extensive experiments on multiple datasets demonstrate that \textbf{WilLaGS} achieves state-of-the-art performance in reconstruction quality and novel view appearance synthesis, while maintaining real-time rendering efficiency.

</details>


### [51] [A comprehensive and trustworthy benchmark of AI methods for change detection in Earth observation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28247)
*Tadej Tomanič,Alice Baudhuin,Jan Sotošek,Jure Brence,Panče Panov,Nikola Simidjievski,Dragi Kocev*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个标准化、开源的基准，用于系统评估地球观测变化检测领域的深度学习方法。作者在十个异构数据集上对十种代表性模型架构（从CNN到ViT）进行了全面分析，统一实验协议，同时评估预测性能和计算效率（参数量、推理延迟）。研究发现：经典架构（如Siamese U-Net）在考虑计算效率时往往优于复杂当代模型，且预训练能带来显著性能提升且不增加推理成本。所有实验资源均公开并符合FAIR原则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地球观测变化检测领域的研究受到不一致的评估协议和仅关注预测精度而忽视计算效率的窄视野所限制，亟需标准化的基准来公平、全面地评估现有SOTA方法。

Method: 构建标准化开源基准，在十个异构变化检测数据集上对十种代表性模型（覆盖CNN到ViT）进行统一实验协议评估，同时比较从零训练与预训练权重的效果，并将预测性能与计算效率（参数量、推理延迟）联合评估。

Result: 优化良好的经典架构（如Siamese U-Net）在考虑计算效率时优于更复杂的当代模型；预训练始终带来显著的性能增益且不增加推理开销。

Conclusion: 经典架构仍具竞争力，计算效率应作为模型评估的重要维度；预训练是低成本高性能的有效策略。所有资源遵循FAIR原则公开，为领域提供可复现的标准化评估基准。

Abstract: Change detection in Earth observation (EO) is critical for monitoring land surface transformations, yet recent research in the field is constrained by inconsistent evaluation protocols and a narrow focus on predictive accuracy without regard for computational efficiency. To address this, we present a standardized, open-source benchmark for evaluating state-of-the-art (SOTA) deep learning methods for Earth observation change detection. We conduct a comprehensive analysis of ten representative model architectures, ranging from convolutional networks (CNNs) to vision transformers (ViTs), across ten heterogeneous change detection datasets. We rigorously evaluate these models with identical experimental protocols, comparing models trained from scratch against those utilizing pre-trained weights. Furthermore, we evaluate predictive performance alongside computational efficiency, including parameter counts and inference latency. Our findings reveal that well-optimized classical architectures, such as Siamese U-Nets, frequently outperform more complex contemporary models when computational efficiency is factored in, and that pre-training consistently provides a significant performance boost with no additional inference cost. To ensure complete transparency and reproducibility, all experimental resources, including standardized data splits, training scripts, training logs, and model checkpoints are publicly available and adhere to FAIR principles (Findable, Accessible, Interoperable, and Reusable).

</details>


### [52] [Synth-JDoc: Synthesizing a Japanese Document Image Dataset for OCR with Diverse Layouts and Embedded Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28248)
*Keito Sasagawa,Shuhei Kurita,Daisuke Kawahara*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文针对大型视觉语言模型（LVLMs）在日文竖排文本阅读能力不足的问题，提出利用HTML和CSS合成包含横竖排的多栏文档图像来构建OCR数据集。通过嵌入文生图模型生成的图像增强视觉真实性，并施加噪声和退化滤镜提升模型鲁棒性。实验证明该合成数据集在提升LVLM日文竖排文本阅读性能上优于先前工作和高性能文生图模型生成的基线数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 日文文档常同时包含竖排和横排文本，而当前LVLMs对竖排日文文本的阅读性能显著低于横排文本。手工构建OCR数据集成本高昂且难以扩展，从现有文档图像提取文本又存在识别错误和图像来源受限等问题，亟需一种低成本、可扩展且高质量的合成OCR数据集来弥补这一差距。

Method: 利用HTML和CSS从文本直接合成文档图像，生成同时包含横排和竖排风格的多栏文档；在版面中嵌入文生图模型生成的图像以增强视觉真实性；对合成文档图像施加噪声和退化滤镜以提升模型鲁棒性。

Result: 实验将基于该合成数据集微调的模型与基于先前工作合成数据集和高性能文生图模型生成的数据集微调的基线模型进行对比，结果表明该合成数据集在提升LVLM日文竖排文本阅读性能方面最为有效。

Conclusion: 该合成数据集方法能够有效提升LVLMs对日文竖排文本的阅读能力，且数据集和代码已公开，为后续相关研究提供了资源基础。

Abstract: The ability of Large Vision Language Models (LVLMs) to read text within document images is crucial, as it enables various applications such as Document Visual Question Answering. To enhance the text-reading capabilities of LVLMs, high-quality OCR datasets are essential. This need is particularly critical for Japanese documents, which often feature vertically written text alongside horizontally written text. Current LVLMs demonstrate considerably lower performance on vertically written Japanese text than on horizontally written text, necessitating specialized OCR datasets to bridge this gap. However, manually constructing OCR datasets is expensive and difficult to scale. Alternatively, constructing datasets by extracting text from existing document images using OCR models introduces challenges, such as text recognition errors and the prerequisite of sourcing document images.
  To address these issues, we construct an OCR dataset by synthesizing document images directly from text. Leveraging HTML and CSS, we generate multi-column documents that incorporate both vertical and horizontal writing styles. Furthermore, to ensure the visual realism of the documents, we embed images generated by text-to-image models within the layout. Additionally, to foster model robustness, we apply noise and degradation filters to the synthesized document images. In our experiments, we compared the performance of models fine-tuned on our synthetic dataset against baselines fine-tuned on synthetic datasets from prior work and those generated by a high-performance text-to-image model. Evaluation results demonstrate that our synthetic dataset is the most effective approach for improving LVLM performance on reading vertically written Japanese text. Our dataset and code are publicly available (https://github.com/llm-jp/synth-jdoc).

</details>


### [53] [Non-Uniform Quantisation for 3DGS Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28272)
*Bert Van hauwermeiren,Patrice Rondao Alface,Adrian Munteanu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向3D高斯溅射（3DGS）模型的非均匀量化压缩方案，通过重要性加权量化和合并消除冗余，实现最先进的压缩性能，并与MPEG 3DGS压缩标准化工作兼容。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3DGS在新型视角合成中表现出色，但其高比特率需求给存储和传输带来重大挑战，需要标准化的压缩格式以确保3DGS生态系统的实用性和互操作性。

Method: 提出一种非均匀量化方案，通过重要性加权量化适应底层数据分布，并通过重要性加权合并消除体素化后的冗余。

Result: 在基准数据集上的广泛评估表明，该方法达到了最先进的压缩性能，且与任何基于点云的表示兼容。

Conclusion: 该方案可作为MPEG 3DGS压缩标准化活动的正式贡献，为3DGS生态系统的实用部署提供支撑。

Abstract: 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) has emerged as a powerful technique for novel view synthesis, yet its high bitrate requirements pose significant challenges for storage and transmission. To enable practical applications and ensure interoperability within the 3DGS ecosystem, standardised compression formats are essential. In this paper, we propose a novel non-uniform quantisation scheme specifically tailored for 3DGS models. Our approach adapts to the underlying data distribution by applying importance-weighted quantisation and eliminating post-voxelisation redundancy through importance weighted merging. Extensive evaluations on benchmark datasets demonstrate that our method achieves state-of-the-art compression performance. Furthermore, the proposed scheme is compatible with any point-cloud-based representation and is intended as a formal contribution to the upcoming MPEG 3DGS compression standardisation activities.

</details>


### [54] [GeoFF3D: Coordinate-Anchored Feed-Forward Reconstruction for Large-Scale UAV Mapping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28288)
*Xiang Yang,Yongli Wang,Yunsheng Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GeoFF3D是一种将坐标锚定模型与空间大规模重建框架（SLRF）相结合的方法，用于大规模无人机航测重建。通过地理参照相机平移和可选的几何先验，在重力对齐的Z-up公制坐标系中直接预测相机位姿和密集点图，实现可扩展、稳健的大规模重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有前馈式3D重建方法通常只能处理有限数量的图像，且在局部或内部归一化坐标系中恢复相机和几何结构。将其扩展到大规模无人机航测需要可扩展的多块处理和可靠的聚合，而完整Sim(3)对齐在近似共线的轨迹上可能不稳定。这些限制促使需要一种能够直接在大规模地理坐标系中工作的重建方法。

Method: GeoFF3D结合两部分：1）坐标锚定模型，使用地理参照相机平移和可选几何先验，直接在重力对齐的Z-up公制坐标系中预测相机位姿和密集点图；2）空间大规模重建框架（SLRF），将图像划分为空间重叠的块，传播共享视图先验，并层次化聚合局部重建，且可适用于不同的有界视图模型。

Result: 在九个航测块上，GeoFF3D取得了最佳平均重建质量，将F@5从Pi3X+SLRF的0.829提升到0.877；在长序列UAVScenes上达到0.848，而Pi3X+SLRF为0.687，最强SLAM/流式基线为0.451。GeoFF3D约5分钟即可重建2000张图像，展示了可扩展性和稳健性。

Conclusion: GeoFF3D通过地理坐标锚定和空间大规模重建框架，解决了现有前馈重建方法在大规模UAV航测中的可扩展性和稳定性问题，显著提升了重建质量并保持了高效的计算性能，代码已开源。

Abstract: Existing feed-forward 3D reconstruction methods typically process a bounded number of images and recover cameras and geometry in local or internally normalized frames. Extending them to large-scale UAV mapping requires scalable multi-chunk processing and reliable aggregation, while full Sim(3) alignment can become unstable for near collinear trajectories. We present GeoFF3D, which combines a coordinate-anchored model with a spatial large-scale reconstruction framework (SLRF). The model uses georeferenced camera translations and optional geometric priors to predict camera poses and dense point maps directly in a gravity-aligned Z-up metric frame. SLRF partitions images into spatially overlapping chunks, propagates shared-view priors, and aggregates local reconstructions hierarchically, while remaining applicable to different bounded-view models. Across nine aerial mapping blocks, GeoFF3D achieves the best average reconstruction quality, improving F@5 from 0.829 for Pi3X + SLRF to 0.877. On long UAVScenes sequences, it reaches 0.848, compared with 0.687 for Pi3X + SLRF and 0.451 for the strongest evaluated SLAM/streaming baseline. GeoFF3D reconstructs 2,000 images in approximately five minutes, demonstrating scalable and robust large-scale UAV reconstruction.The code is available at https://github.com/yanxian-ll/GeoFF3D.

</details>


### [55] [FUSED: Forensic-Semantic Mixture-of-Experts for AI Inpainting Detection and Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28302)
*Anton Nuzhdin,Marcel Worring,Ivona Najdenkoska*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FUSED是一个统一的框架，用于联合检测和定位AI生成图像的修复（inpainting）区域，采用稀疏门控的专家混合架构结合低级取证线索与高级语义特征，在跨生成器基准上取得了最佳检测和定位性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型的图像修复仅修改图像的局部区域，而现有AI图像检测器依赖全局伪影且不具备定位能力。伪影在不同生成器间存在差异导致检测器在分布偏移下性能下降，且外部位像素修复会破坏检测线索，因此需要同时兼顾检测与定位的统一框架。

Method: FUSED采用稀疏门控的专家混合（MoE）架构，将低层取证线索与高层语义特征相结合，通过门控机制为每个token自适应地选择最相关的信号。模型同时预测图像级别的操作分数和像素级别的修复区域掩码。

Result: 在OpenSDID跨生成器基准上，FUSED取得了最佳的平均检测与定位性能，在未见过的生成器上提升最大。该模型可直接迁移至AutoSplice和CocoGlide基准，定位性能提升超过两倍。消融实验表明所有方法（包括FUSED在内）都会部分利用全局生成器伪影作为操作证据，但FUSED在有无该伪影的条件下均为最强方法。

Conclusion: FUSED通过融合多层级特征与稀疏门控专家混合架构，有效解决了AI图像修复的检测与定位问题，在跨生成器泛化方面表现优异，并展示了在不同基准间的直接迁移能力。

Abstract: Diffusion-based inpainting models modify only a localized part of an image, while many AI-image detectors rely on global artifacts and do not localize. These artifacts vary across generators, limiting detector transfer under distribution shifts. Recent work shows that restoring the authentic pixels outside the inpainted region removes these cues and can degrade pretrained detectors. To address this, we present FUSED, a unified framework for the joint detection and localization of AI-generated inpainting. FUSED combines low-level forensic cues with high-level semantic features using a sparsely-gated Mixture-of-Experts architecture, enabling the model to adaptively prioritize the most relevant signal for each token. For each input, FUSED predicts both an image-level manipulation score and a pixel-level mask of the inpainted area. On the OpenSDID cross-generator benchmark, FUSED achieves the best average detection and localization, with the largest gains on unseen generators. The same model transfers directly to the held-out AutoSplice and CocoGlide benchmarks, more than doubling localization performance. Evaluating each held-out benchmark with and without the global generator artifact further shows that all evaluated methods, ours included, partly read the artifact as evidence of manipulation, and FUSED remains the strongest under both conditions. Code and pretrained models are available at https://github.com/AntonNuzhdin/FUSED.

</details>


### [56] [AIM: Anchor Identity Features, Then Match for Multimodal Large Language Model Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28312)
*Wonjun Lee,Jaehyuk Jang,Kangwook Ko,Hee-Seon Kim,Changick Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）中的身份遗忘问题，在删除时无可用的保留图像这一现实约束下，提出AIM方法——通过通用视觉提示锚定身份遗忘目标，并在Fisher约束下匹配视觉编码器，实现高效的特定身份遗忘同时保留视觉感知能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MLLMs在微调数据中会记忆人物身份相关的特定事实，带来隐私风险。现有的MLLM遗忘方法通常假设删除时可获得保留图像或真实答案，这在实际场景中不现实。作者研究在删除时无法获得保留图像的情况下的身份遗忘问题，探索如何在不破坏通用视觉感知的前提下实现身份信息遗忘。

Method: 作者首先通过分析发现身份问题与视觉感知问题在微调后的隐藏状态中占据不同区域，且组织方式不同：身份问题按人物聚类，感知问题按问题类型聚类。基于此观察，提出两阶段方法AIM：第一阶段用通用视觉提示锚定身份遗忘目标；第二阶段在Fisher信息约束下将视觉编码器匹配到该目标，从而在不擦除通用视觉感知的情况下抑制身份知识。

Result: 大量实验表明AIM实现了具有竞争力的身份遗忘效果，同时保持了非删除身份、先验知识和同一图像上的视觉感知能力。

Conclusion: 身份知识和视觉感知在MLLMs中具有可分离的隐藏状态结构，AIM利用这一特性在无保留图像的现实场景下实现了有效的身份遗忘，兼顾了隐私保护与模型功能性保留。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) can memorize identity-specific facts about people in their fine-tuning data, creating privacy risks when a person requests deletion. Existing MLLM unlearning methods often assume access to retain images or ground-truth answers during deletion, which is unrealistic in many practical scenarios. We study identity unlearning when retain images are unavailable at deletion time. Our analysis shows that identity and visual-perception questions occupy distinct regions in fine-tuned hidden states and are organized differently: identity questions cluster by person, whereas perception questions cluster by question type. This suggests that identity knowledge can be suppressed without erasing general visual perception. Building on this observation, we propose AIM, a two-stage method that anchors an identity-forgetting target with a universal visual prompt and then matches the vision encoder to that target under a Fisher-based constraint. Extensive experiments show that AIM achieves competitive identity forgetting while preserving non-deleted identities, prior knowledge, and visual perception on the same images.

</details>


### [57] [Conditional Visual Evidence Utility: State-Dependent Rank Reversals in Frozen Vision-Language Encoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28316)
*Yunxuan Fang,Xinhe Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文通过受控的视觉组合搜索实验，证明了视觉语言模型中证据的重要性是状态依赖的——在观察到某个线索后，剩余线索的边际效用会发生变化。研究发现OpenCLIP和SigLIP表现出稳健的排名逆转现象，且这种逆转仅在候选重叠区域集中出现，并在对查询-场景进行打乱后消失。重排证据顺序能带来决策价值，表明应改用条件性而非静态排名来评估视觉证据的使用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统方法使用静态重要性分数将视觉证据压缩为单一排名，但这种做法忽略了证据价值会随已观察线索而动态变化的事实。作者希望在受控环境中系统研究这种状态依赖的排名逆转是否真实存在，以及是否影响下游决策。

Method: 在受控组合视觉搜索任务中，独立暴露颜色、形状和纹理三类证据，测量不同获取状态下各线索的条件边际效用。使用800个场景进行验证，跨两种证据累积构造和十种等效查询措辞测试，并采用查询-场景打乱作为对照。最后通过匹配首次动作的探索性分析检验重排对决策效用（step-2 utility）的影响。

Result: 冻结的OpenCLIP和SigLIP均表现出稳健的状态依赖排名逆转，且集中在候选重叠区域。该结构在两种累积构造和十种查询措辞下均存在，但在打乱条件（derangement）下消失。重排仅在首次获取后即产生正向的step-2效用，且跨证据模式、措辞和骨干网络评估时依然成立。

Conclusion: 证据重要性在受控视觉搜索中确实具有状态依赖性，更新证据排序能保持跨评估场景的决策价值。这提示应当以条件性方式评估视觉语言模型的证据使用，而非依赖单一静态排名，并为未来自适应性证据选择方法提供了可量化的评估目标。

Abstract: Static importance scores compress visual evidence into a single ranking, but the value of remaining evidence can change after one cue has been observed. We study this possibility in controlled compositional visual search, where color, shape, and texture evidence can be independently exposed and their conditional marginal utility measured across acquisition states. In a held-out confirmation on 800 scenes, frozen OpenCLIP and SigLIP exhibit robust state-dependent rank reversals that concentrate in candidate-overlap regimes designed to induce ordering changes. The structure persists across two evidence-accumulation constructions and ten equivalent query wordings, but disappears under query-scene derangement. We also ask whether these reversals matter for decisions. In a post-confirmation exploratory matched-first-action analysis, reranking only after the first acquisition yields positive step-2 utility when decisions are selected under one evidence mode, wording, or backbone and evaluated under another. Together, these results show that evidence importance is state-dependent in this controlled setup and that updating an evidence ordering can retain decision-relevant value across evaluator changes. They motivate evaluating vision-language evidence use conditionally rather than through a single static ranking, while providing a measurable target for future adaptive evidence-selection methods.

</details>


### [58] [Abstract4D: A Large-Scale Dataset and Framework for Understanding the Visual Language of Abstract Art](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28339)
*Haowei Zhang,Yuanpei Zhao,Ji-Zhe Zhou,Mao Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文介绍了Abstract4D，一个包含超过12万幅抽象画作及其元数据和多维提示词（涵盖形式、色彩、纹理和构图）的最大抽象绘画数据集。通过人类与视觉语言模型混合标注流程构建，该数据集支持嵌入可视化分析、分类、跨模态检索和文生图等基准任务，用于评估AI感知和再现抽象视觉语言的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI虽然能对艺术风格进行分类和图像合成，但缺乏对赋予艺术意义的视觉语言的建模。抽象绘画最小化了物体语义、突出了结构线索，是计算感知的理想测试平台，因此需要大规模的高质量数据集来系统研究AI如何理解和再现抽象艺术。

Method: 构建Abstract4D数据集，包含120,000+抽象画作，通过人类与视觉语言模型（VLM）混合标注流程生成多维感知属性提示词（形式、色彩、纹理、构图）。利用嵌入可视化分析抽象艺术的语义结构，并建立分类、跨模态检索和文生图三大基准任务。

Result: 通过大规模嵌入可视化揭示了抽象艺术中感知关系如何组织艺术意义；建立了涵盖分类、跨模态检索和文生图的基准任务，可定量评估AI模型感知和再现抽象视觉语言的能力。

Conclusion: Abstract4D为AI理解和解释抽象艺术提供了既可探索又可量化评估的平台，有助于推动AI对抽象视觉语言的表示与诠释能力研究。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence can classify artistic styles and synthesize images, but it still lacks a model of the visual language that gives art meaning. Abstract painting minimizes object semantics and foregrounds structural cues, making it an ideal testbed for computational perception. We introduce \textbf{Abstract4D}, the largest dataset of abstract paintings to date: more than 120,000 images paired with rich metadata and multi-dimensional prompts that capture each work's perceptual attributes---\textit{form, color, texture, and composition}. Annotations are produced by a hybrid human--VLM pipeline for quality and consistency. Using Abstract4D, we (i) analyze the semantic structure of abstract art through large-scale embedding visualization, uncovering how perceptual relationships organize artistic meaning, and (ii) establish benchmark tasks for classification, cross-modal retrieval, and text-to-image generation to evaluate how AI models perceive and reproduce abstract visual language. Together, these analyses demonstrate how Abstract4D enables both exploration and quantitative assessment of AI's ability to represent and interpret abstract art.

</details>


### [59] [Cross-Spectral Dense Correspondence for Multimodal Spectral Medical Imaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28341)
*Eric L. Wisotzky,Jost Triller,Simon W. Härtl,Oliver T. Bruns,Peter Eisert,Anna Hilsmann*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文针对多模态光谱成像系统中跨谱段密集对应匹配的数据缺口问题，提出了传感器无关的跨光谱调制协议和合成跨光谱对应基准，在现有密集对应骨干网络上显著提升了严重光谱失配下的匹配性能，同时保持RGB基准上的原有表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态光谱成像系统在医学和科学成像中需要精确的密集对应关系来融合不同波长范围的数据，但不同光谱通道间存在对比度变化、强度反转和外观偏移，且难以获取密集真值，传统RGB训练数据的监督能力有限，形成了数据缺口。

Method: 提出传感器无关的跨光谱调制协议，在既有对应基准上通过强度输入投影进行数据增强；同时构建模拟物理合理辐射差异的合成跨光谱对应基准，用于训练和评估。

Result: 在多种现代密集对应骨干网络上应用统一的跨光谱协议训练后，严重光谱失配场景下性能显著提升，且RGB基准性能保持稳定。消融实验表明视角相关通道选择和非线性辐射变换提供互补的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 现有模型的局限性主要在于训练数据分布与目标光谱特征之间的不匹配，而非结构匹配能力不足。该训练数据增强协议可作为异构医学光谱采集系统中实现空间相干光谱融合的有效使能器。

Abstract: Precise dense correspondence is a fundamental prerequisite for multimodal spectral imaging systems that fuse disparate wavelength ranges for subsequent analysis in medical and scientific imaging. Corresponding image points are often observed with non-overlapping spectral sensitivities, leading to wavelength-dependent contrast changes, intensity inversions, and appearance shifts for which dense ground truth is difficult to obtain and conventional RGB-based training data provides only limited supervision. We address this data gap by introducing a sensor-agnostic cross-spectral modulation protocol on established correspondence benchmarks with intensity input projection, and by proposing a synthetic cross-spectral correspondence benchmark simulating physically plausible radiometric differences. Evaluation on several modern dense correspondence backbones trained with our unified cross-spectral protocol showed substantial improvements under severe spectral mismatch while maintaining performance on standard RGB benchmarks. Ablation experiments show that view-dependent channel selection and nonlinear radiometric transformations provide complementary robustness, indicating that the primary limitation of existing models is not their structural matching capacity but the mismatch between training distribution and spectral characteristics of the target image pair. Qualitative evaluations on heterogeneous medical spectral acquisition systems demonstrate the practical relevance of the proposed training data augmentation protocol as an enabler for spatially coherent spectral fusion in HSI workflows.

</details>


### [60] [Denoising-Aware Temporal Point Cloud Completion for 3D Crop Architecture Recovery and Phenotypic Trait Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28343)
*Mrudul Mittal,Soumyashree Kar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了SynthCrop4D，一个程序化生成的时序植物点云合成数据集，提供可控噪声、遮挡和完整几何真值，用于评估两阶段时空修复管线（空间去噪+时序补全），在合成和真实数据上均验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高通量表型分析依赖于跨生长阶段准确的植物3D重建，但时序补全方法的开发和评估受限于缺乏具有完整几何真值的数据集。

Method: 构建SynthCrop4D程序化合成数据集，提出两阶段管线：先用去噪模块移除激光扫描点云中的结构伪影，再用自适应时序PoinTr模型利用前一时段(t-1)信息重建当前生长阶段(t)的点云，以恢复自遮挡导致的缺失区域。

Result: 去噪显著提升重建质量，最佳配置在SynthCrop4D上Chamfer Distance达0.0061（Temporal PoinTr + Mamba-DG），在Pheno4D上F-Score达0.2080（Vanilla PoinTr + Mamba-DG）；用于表型提取时凸包体积MAE在合成数据上为0.021，真实数据上为0.343。

Conclusion: SynthCrop4D数据集和所提出的两阶段管线为时序植物重建和高通量作物表型分析提供了基准和方法论。

Abstract: High-throughput phenotyping depends on accurate 3D reconstruction of plants across growth stages, yet the development and evaluation of temporal completion methods are limited by the lack of datasets with complete geometric ground truth. To address this challenge, we introduce SynthCrop4D, a procedurally generated synthetic dataset of temporally evolving plant point clouds that provides controllable noise, occlusion, and complete plant geometry for benchmarking reconstruction methods. Using this dataset, we evaluate a two-stage pipeline that combines spatial denoising and temporal point cloud completion. First, a denoising module removes structural artifacts from raw laser-scanned point clouds. The resulting data are then processed by an Adaptive Temporal PoinTr model that reconstructs the current growth stage (t) using information from the previous stage (t-1), enabling recovery of regions missing due to self-occlusion. We evaluate the proposed framework on both SynthCrop4D and the real-world Pheno4D dataset (tomato and maize) under settings with and without denoising. Results show that denoising substantially improves reconstruction quality, with the best configuration achieving a Chamfer Distance of 0.0061 on SynthCrop4D (Temporal PoinTr + Mamba-DG) and an F-Score of 0.2080 on Pheno4D (Vanilla PoinTr + Mamba-DG). We further demonstrate the use of completed point clouds for phenotypic trait extraction, including plant height, canopy width, and convex hull volume, obtaining hull-volume MAEs of 0.021 on synthetic data and 0.343 on real data. Together, SynthCrop4D and the proposed pipeline provide a benchmark and methodology for temporal plant reconstruction and high-throughput crop phenotyping.

</details>


### [61] [Real-Time Musculoskeletal Surrogates for Pediatric Cerebral Palsy: a Credibility Pilot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28371)
*Mohammad Arif Ul Alam*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文开发了一种基于主体的因果神经替代模型，用于脑瘫儿童实时肌肉骨骼代理，在9名儿童数据集上实现高精度肌腱长度预测（R²≈0.92-0.95，nRMSE<8%）且推断延迟远低于100ms交互目标，同时通过蒙特卡洛可信度试验揭示了力预测和不确定性校准方面的关键挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实时肌肉骨骼代理有望支持脑瘫儿童个性化康复，但其临床可信度取决于主体级评估、低推断延迟和校准的不确定性——这些在当前小规模异质数据集中尚未得到充分验证。

Method: 构建主体条件的因果神经替代模型，整合OpenSim静态参数、时间关节运动学、真实肌肉能力及训练扰动；使用留一主体交叉验证于6名开发对象，并在3名锁定测试对象上评估冻结配置；通过蒙特卡洛模拟传播±5%人体测量和肌肉能力变异来测试可信度。

Result: 肌腱长度准确复现（开发验证R²=0.92，锁定主体约0.95，nRMSE<8%），神经推断仅需亚毫秒至数毫秒；直接肌力估计不稳定，池化指标可能夸大主体内逐肌准确性；蒙特卡洛试验显示仅±5%变异传播就导致名义90%区间严重过度自信（力覆盖率约4%，肌腱长度覆盖率<1%）。

Conclusion: 建立了针对儿科肌肉骨骼替代模型的无泄漏评估与可信度框架，确认肌力建模和认知不确定性是迈向临床可信数字孪生的核心挑战。

Abstract: Real-time musculoskeletal (MSK) surrogates could support personalized rehabilitation for children with cerebral palsy (CP), but their credibility depends on subject-wise evaluation, low inference latency, and calibrated uncertainty. We develop a subject-conditioned causal neural surrogate using OpenSim-derived static parameters, temporal joint kinematics, true muscle capacities, and training-only perturbations. On a real pediatric CP gait dataset comprising nine children, we use leave-one-subject-out validation on six development subjects and evaluate a frozen configuration once on three locked test subjects. The surrogate accurately reproduces musculotendon lengths (R-square = 0.92 in development validation and approximately 0.95 on locked subjects; nRMSE < 8%) while requiring only sub-millisecond to few-millisecond neural inference, well below a 100 ms interactive-rehabilitation target. In contrast, direct muscle-force estimation remains unstable at this small, heterogeneous scale: pooled metrics can overstate within-subject, per-muscle accuracy. A Monte Carlo credibility pilot further shows that propagating only +/-5% anthropometry and muscle-capacity variation produces severely overconfident nominal 90% intervals (approximately 4% force coverage and below 1% MT-length coverage). These results establish a leakage-free evaluation and credibility framework for pediatric MSK surrogates, while identifying force modeling and epistemic uncertainty as the central next challenges for clinically credible digital twins.

</details>


### [62] [Semantic Head Specialization Guides Hybrid ViT Attention for Multimodal LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28383)
*Chenhong He,Lei Li,Shicheng Li,Hanglong Lv,Lingpeng Kong,Qi Liu,Tong Yang,Shuhuai Ren*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究了多模态大语言模型中视觉Transformer（ViT）的注意力头分化现象，提出语义头特化（SHS）概念及其量化指标SHS-Index，并基于三个结构因素设计出混合注意力机制Ariadne Attention，在6.5倍更少的注意力计算下匹配全注意力的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合注意力在前沿大语言模型中占据主导地位，但多模态LLM中的ViT缺乏令人满意的混合注意力设计，且学界对为何某些注意力模式效果更好缺乏共识。作者希望填补这一空白，通过理解ViT注意力头的分化规律来指导混合注意力的设计。

Method: 首先研究ViT注意力头，发现它们分化为物体专家和背景专家角色（即语义头特化SHS），并据此提出量化指标SHS-Index。随后利用该指标区分全注意力和分块窗口ViT，并识别出三个影响SHS的结构因素：窗口交互、token序列化和局部softmax分配。最后基于这三个设计原则提出Ariadne Attention混合注意力机制。

Result: SHS-Index能够有效区分全注意力与分块窗口ViT，并且与下游基准性能强相关。基于三个结构因素设计的Ariadne Attention在22个图像和视频任务上匹配全注意力性能，同时将注意力计算量降低至原来的约1/6.5。

Conclusion: 注意力头特化是一个可量化的属性，可用于诊断和设计多模态LLM规模下的原则性混合ViT注意力机制，为混合注意力设计提供了新的理论依据和方法论。

Abstract: Hybrid attention dominates frontier LLMs, yet Vision Transformers (ViTs) in multimodal LLMs lack a satisfactory hybrid design, with no consensus on why certain attention patterns work better. To fill this gap, we study ViT attention heads and find they differentiate into object- and background-specialist roles, a pattern most pronounced under full attention; we call this Semantic Head Specialization (SHS). We propose SHS-Index to quantify this specialization, show that it distinguishes full-attention from chunk-window ViTs, and find that it strongly tracks downstream benchmark performance. We then identify three structural factors that shape SHS---window interaction, token serialization, and local softmax allocation---and use them as design principles for hybrid attention. Guided by these factors, we design Ariadne Attention, a hybrid that matches full attention on 22 image and video tasks at 6.5x less attention compute. Our findings establish head specialization as a measurable property for diagnosing and designing principled hybrid ViT attention at the multimodal-LLM scale.

</details>


### [63] [GraspHOI: Full-Body 3D Human-Object Reconstruction with Finger-Level Grasps from a Single In-the-Wild Image](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28386)
*Semin Kim,Haechan Shin,Jongyoo Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GraspHOI是首个从单张图像重建全身3D人-物交互（HOI）的框架，它显式地优化手指关节形态以匹配重建出的物体，实现了类别无关的物体重建与手指级抓握优化相结合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的单目全身3D人-物交互方法没有将显式的手指级抓握优化与类别无关的物体重建结合起来。尽管身体-物体姿态看起来合理，但手指可能悬浮于物体表面或穿透物体，而非形成真实抓握。

Method: GraspHOI分别重建身体、手和物体，通过基于深度的配准和图像空间的对齐将它们统一到公制相机空间中。利用遮挡感知的掌侧对应关系将物体贴合到抓握手，并通过接触感知优化细化手臂和手指的关节形态，形成表面接触的同时避免过度穿透。

Result: 在四个基准数据集和六个基线方法上，GraspHOI在人-物相对位置、手部准确性和接触合理性方面均有改进。

Conclusion: GraspHOI证明将显式的手指级抓握优化与类别无关的物体重建相结合，能够有效提升单目全身3D人-物交互重建的质量，完整代码将开源发布。

Abstract: Existing monocular full-body 3D human-object interaction (HOI) methods do not combine explicit finger-level grasp optimization with category-agnostic object reconstruction. Despite plausible body-object configurations, their fingers may float from or penetrate objects instead of forming a grasp. We present GraspHOI, the first framework that reconstructs a full-body 3D HOI from a single image while explicitly optimizing finger articulation against the reconstructed object. GraspHOI recovers object geometry directly, without predefined meshes or a fixed category vocabulary. It reconstructs the body, hands, and object separately, aligning them in metric camera space via depth-based registration and image-space alignment. Occlusion-aware palmar correspondences seat the object against the grasping hand, and contact-aware optimization refines arm and finger articulation to form surface contact without excessive penetration. Across four benchmarks and six baselines, GraspHOI improves relative human-object placement, hand accuracy, and contact plausibility. Full pipeline code will be released.

</details>


### [64] [How Far Can 5,500 Hours of Driving Take You? A Scaling Law Analysis of Video Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28404)
*Victor Besnier,Anh-Quan Cao,Elias Ramzi,Spyros Gidaris,Tuan-Hung Vu,Andrei Bursuc,Eloi Zablocki,Matthieu Cord*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文针对自动驾驶视频生成无法采用网络级数据扩展路线的问题（驾驶数据昂贵、受隐私限制且无法随意爬取），提出了一个系统性的缩放定律研究。研究涵盖了从1M到9B参数的视频扩散模型族，在不同训练曝光下使用多达5500小时驾驶数据进行训练。验证损失在模型规模和训练曝光方面均遵循一致的幂律，并据此训练了一个9B参数模型——据我们所知这是从零开始在驾驶数据上训练的最大视频扩散模型，在nuScenes上创下了开源驾驶视频生成的新SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶视频生成无法像网络数据那样大规模获取数据，因为驾驶数据采集成本高、受隐私约束且不能随意抓取，因此模型必须最大化利用有限的固定语料。这一现实约束使得传统的"数据越多越好"路线不可行，需要系统性地理解如何在固定数据下最优配置计算资源、模型规模和训练时长。

Method: 从零开始训练一组参数从1M到9B的视频扩散模型，在高达5500小时驾驶数据上以不同训练曝光进行实验。通过验证损失建立模型规模和训练曝光两个维度的幂律关系，分析计算最优的缩放策略。

Result: 验证损失在模型规模和训练曝光上均遵循一致的幂律。损失随训练曝光的改善速度远快于随模型规模的改善速度，因此在有限计算下延长训练是改进固定模型最有效的方式；但更大模型能达到更低的渐近损失，当计算和数据充足时，计算最优缩放仍然偏向增大模型规模。基于这些定律训练的9B参数模型在nuScenes上创下了开源驾驶视频生成的新SOTA。

Conclusion: 研究提供了在固定数据限制下分配自动驾驶视频生成训练预算的实用指南：计算有限时优先延长训练，计算和/or数据充裕时应增大模型规模以获得更低损失。同时验证了缩放定律在驾驶视频生成这一数据受限场景中的适用性，并发布了最大的驾驶数据视频扩散模型及代码。

Abstract: Video generation for autonomous driving cannot follow the web-scale route: driving data is expensive to collect, bound by privacy requirements, and cannot be scraped at will, so models must make the most of a fixed corpus. We present a systematic scaling-law study of video diffusion models trained from scratch on driving data: a family of models from 1M to 9B parameters, trained at different exposures on up to 5,500 hours of driving. Validation loss follows consistent power laws in both model size and training exposure, answering the questions that shape a training budget: whether compute is better spent on longer training or on a larger model, and whether more data is needed. Loss improves much faster with training exposure than with model size, making longer training the most effective way to improve a fixed model under limited compute. However, larger models continue to achieve lower asymptotic loss, so compute-optimal scaling still favors increasing model size when sufficient compute and data are available. Guided by these laws, we train a 9B-parameter model, to our knowledge the largest video diffusion model trained from scratch on driving data: it sets a new open-source state of the art for driving video generation, as measured on nuScenes. Our code and pretrained models are available at https://github.com/valeoai/VATIX. NATIX is separately releasing the underlying driving data in stages.

</details>


### [65] [Post-Training VLMs for Video Mistake Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28406)
*Federico Spurio,Olga Zatsarynna,Lars Doorenbos,Emad Bahrami,Gianpiero Francesca,Juergen Gall*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向视频中指令执行错误检测的新协议MD-VQA及其基准测试，并首次提出用于错误检测的视频语言模型后训练技术，通过定制奖励函数引导模型识别指令与视频之间的不一致性。实验表明该方法优于零样本、监督微调和后训练基线，在未见程序上的泛化性能尤为突出（最高提升11.6%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频错误检测方法主要基于封闭集协议，一旦任务变化就需要重新收集数据和训练模型，限制了其广泛适用性。作者主张错误检测方法应学习"错误"的一般概念而非过拟合步骤特定细节，从而提出了更通用的开放集错误检测协议和基准。

Method: 提出MD-VQA协议及配套基准，测试模型能否判断一个步骤是否按描述正确执行（涵盖已知和未知动作）。在此基础上，提出首个面向错误检测的视频语言模型后训练技术，使用定制的奖励函数鼓励模型识别指令与对应视频之间的差异。

Result: 大量实验表明，该方法优于零样本、监督微调和后训练基线。在EP-VQA基准上，对于未见程序的泛化能力尤为突出，相比最佳基线提升高达11.6%。

Conclusion: 该工作为通用错误检测（general mistake detection）铺平了道路，验证了学习"错误"通用概念而非步骤特定细节的有效性。代码和基准已公开。

Abstract: Human mistakes are inevitable when following instructions, yet they can lead to severe consequences. As such, there has been an increased interest in developing methods for detecting mistakes in videos, with current methods mostly focusing on closed-set protocols. While successful in controlled settings, the closed-set assumption limits their wider applicability, as any changes to the task require collecting new data and re-training models. Instead, we argue that mistake detection methods should learn the general concept of a mistake, rather than overfitting to step-specific details. To reflect this, we introduce the Mistake Detection Video Question Answering (MD-VQA) protocol and accompanying benchmark. MD-VQA tests whether methods can discern if a step was executed correctly with respect to its description, for both seen and unseen actions. To address this important challenge, we propose the first video-language-model post-training technique for mistake detection. Our method uses a tailored reward function to encourage the model to identify discrepancies between an instruction and the corresponding video. Extensive evaluations demonstrate that this approach outperforms zero-shot, supervised fine-tuning, and post-training baselines. Notably, our method generalizes especially well to unseen procedures, for instance, with an improvement of up to 11.6% over the best-performing baseline on EP-VQA, paving the way toward general mistake detection. We release our code and benchmark at https://github.com/FedeSpu/mstk.

</details>


### [66] [Lossy Event Compression: From Event Stream Distortion to Task Performance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28429)
*Zahra Rezaee,Catarina Brites,João Ascenso*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文针对事件相机在高动态场景下产生海量事件数据导致的带宽和存储问题，提出了两种事件压缩管线（基于聚合直方图的JPEG 2000管线和基于点云的G-PCC管线），并在统一的任务驱动评估框架下，首次将五种基于分类的失真度量应用于事件压缩领域，实验证明这些度量能可靠预测任务级性能退化，可作为重复任务评估的高效替代方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 事件相机在中等至高运动场景下每秒可产生数亿个事件，带来严重的带宽和存储挑战，损失压缩必不可少。然而，现有事件流失真度量无法在任务层面可靠预测压缩导致的性能退化，迫使编码器优化依赖昂贵且耗时的任务特定评估，亟需一种高效可靠的失真度量方法。

Method: 本文构建了两种截然不同的事件压缩管线：1）基于聚合的管线，将事件流转换为极性直方图帧，使用传统图像编码器JPEG 2000压缩；2）基于无帧点云的管线，将事件以3D点形式原生编码，使用基于八叉树的G-PCC编码器。两种管线在统一的任务驱动评估框架下，在四个代表性任务（视频重建、目标检测、光流估计、异步特征跟踪）上进行评估，并首次将五种基于分类的失真度量应用于事件压缩，与现有事件流度量进行基准比较。

Result: 实验结果表明，所提出的失真度量能够在不同编码框架下可靠预测压缩引起的任务性能退化，超越了现有事件流度量指标的预测能力，验证了事件流失真评估可作为重复任务特定评估的高效替代方案。

Conclusion: 本文证明了基于分类的事件失真度量能够有效指导事件数据编码方案的开发与优化，为未来事件数据编码解决方案提供了直接的优化方向，事件流失真评估有望成为实际部署中任务级评估的高效替代途径。

Abstract: Event cameras generate asynchronous, sparse data streams with microsecond temporal resolution, but in moderate-to-high motion scenes they can produce as many as hundreds of millions of events per second, creating significant bandwidth and storage challenges. Lossy compression is therefore essential for practical deployment, yet existing event stream distortion metrics fail to reliably predict compression-induced degradation at the task level, forcing codec optimization to rely on expensive task-specific evaluations. To address this gap, this paper introduces two fundamentally different event compression pipelines: i) an aggregation-based pipeline that converts the event stream into polarity-based histogram frames for compression with the conventional image codec JPEG 2000, and ii) a frame-free point cloud-based pipeline that codes events natively as 3D points using the octree-based codec G-PCC. Both pipelines are then assessed within a unified task-driven evaluation framework that relates event stream distortion to downstream application performance across four representative tasks: i) video reconstruction, ii) object detection, iii) optical flow estimation, and a delay-sensitive task iv) asynchronous feature tracking under a reference-relative protocol. Building on this framework, five classification-based distortion metrics are applied to event compression for the first time, to the best of the authors' knowledge, and benchmarked against existing event stream metrics. Experimental results demonstrate that the proposed metrics reliably predict compression-induced task degradation across different coding frameworks. This demonstrates that event stream distortion assessment can be an efficient alternative to repeated task-specific evaluation, providing direct guidance for the development and optimization of future event data coding solutions.

</details>


### [67] [Prompt-Guided Interactive Segmentation of Interstitial Lung Disease in Thoracic CT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28453)
*Vasilis Dedousis,Lubnaa Abdur Rahman,Lorenzo Brigatο,Ethan Dack,Andreas Christe,Christoph Frank,Manuela Funke-Chambour,Justus Roos,Adrian Huber,Lukas Ebner,Stavroula Mougiakakou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文首次将 MedSAM2 模型适配到交互式3D间质性肺病（ILD）分割任务中，在胸部CT上系统比较了多种微调策略和多种临床提示方式（边界框、点、套索、涂鸦），并在七种ILD模式及健康肺组织数据集上验证了效果。全模型微调效果最佳，平均Dice分数较基线MedSAM2提升4.7个百分点，并验证了由自动分割先验初始化后经放射科医生提示细化的端到端工作流程的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ILD模式的分割对疾病定量评估和纵向监测至关重要，但现有方法依赖密集标注且产生静态预测，无法进行后续修正。尽管可提示模型在交互式分割中展现出潜力，但其在ILD领域的适配尚未被探索，因此本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 提出了MedSAM2在交互式3D ILD分割中的首次适配方案，探索了三种微调策略（包括全模型微调），并评估了四种临床相关的提示方式：边界框、点、套索和涂鸦。同时构建了一个概念验证的端到端工作流，先由自动分割模型生成先验，再由放射科医生通过提示进行细化修正。

Result: 在涵盖七种ILD模式和健康肺组织的数据集上，全模型微调策略表现最佳，平均Dice分数较MedSAM2基线提升4.7个百分点。边界框提示性能最强，而套索和涂鸦等非MedSAM2原生交互方式同样被证明有效。

Conclusion: MedSAM2经过适当的微调后能够有效适配交互式3D ILD分割任务，多种提示方式均可实现有效细化，且基于自动分割先验加放射科医生提示细化的端到端工作流具有实际可行性。模型权重和插件已开源。

Abstract: Accurate segmentation of interstitial lung disease (ILD) patterns is essential for quantitative disease assessment and longitudinal monitoring. However, existing approaches remain limited by relying on dense annotations and producing static predictions that cannot be refined, motivating interactive approaches. While promptable models show promise in interactive segmentation, their adaptation to ILDs remains largely unexplored. To address this gap, we investigate prompt-guided foundation models for ILD refinement and present, to the best of our knowledge, the first adaptation of MedSAM2 for interactive 3D ILD segmentation on thoracic CT. We investigate three fine-tuning strategies and multiple clinically motivated prompts: bounding-boxes (BBox), point, lasso, and scribble. On a dataset spanning seven ILD patterns and healthy lung tissue, full model fine-tuning performed best, improving the average Dice score by 4.7 percentage points over MedSAM2.While BBox prompts achieve the strongest performance, non-native MedSAM2 interactions such as lasso and scribble prompts also prove effective. Finally, we present and evaluate a proof-of-concept end-to-end workflow in which MedSAM2 is initialized from an automatic segmentation prior and subsequently refined using radiologist prompts. Model weights and plug-ins made available at: https://github.com/AIHNlab/ILD-SemiSegTool.

</details>


### [68] [ARC-CT: Anatomy-Routed Contrastive Vision-Language Learning for 3D Chest CT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28455)
*Huseyin Umut Isik,Mehmet Alp Ozaydin,Sila Kurugol,Şeyda Ertekin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ARC-CT是一种面向3D胸部CT的区域感知对比学习框架，通过解剖路由的查询定位、标签Jaccard软InfoNCE目标和器官级对齐损失，仅利用LLM从报告中提取的标签（无需人工标注或边界框），在18种异常分类上以紧凑的ResNet-18骨干网络达到0.86的mask-free宏AUC，超越同类高效基线和更大规模的Transformer模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的全局对比学习用于胸部CT与放射报告的配对学习时存在两个根本问题：一是许多关键异常体积小或局限于特定解剖位置，整卷池化为单一嵌入会稀释其视觉证据；二是标准对比目标将批内所有其他扫描视为负样本，而胸部CT常共享异常，导致共同阳性配对被错误推开，损害学习效果。

Method: ARC-CT包含三个核心组件：(1) AnatomyQFormer——通过自动生成的器官掩膜约束查询来定位异常证据；(2) 标签Jaccard软InfoNCE目标——将标准one-hot目标与每对样本的标签集重叠度融合，减少共享临床发现样本间的假阴性惩罚；(3) 器官级对齐损失——将掩膜池化的视觉特征与离线提取的器官特定报告文本对齐。

Result: ARC-CT在18种异常分类上使用紧凑的3D ResNet-18骨干网络实现了0.86的mask-free宏AUC，不仅优于同量级的高效基线方法，还超过了多个更大的Transformer模型。

Conclusion: 通过解剖路由的区域感知设计和软化的对比学习目标，ARC-CT有效解决了胸部CT中小病灶稀释和共阳性误罚的问题，证明仅依赖LLM提取的标签即可实现高性能的mask-free异常分类，为医学影像领域的高效自监督学习提供了新思路。

Abstract: Contrastive vision-language learning uses paired chest CT volumes and radiology reports to learn abnormality classifiers without manually annotated labels. However, two characteristics of chest CT challenge conventional global contrastive learning. First, many critical abnormalities are small or anatomically localized, and pooling an en- tire volume into a single embedding may dilute their visual evidence. Second, the standard contrastive objective treats every other scan in a batch as a negative. Because many chest CTs share abnormalities, this objective incorrectly pushes co-positive pairs apart. We propose Anatomy-Routed Contrastive Learning for 3D Chest CT (ARC-CT), a region-aware framework that addresses these limitations using only la- bels extracted from reports by an LLM, with no manual annotations or bounding boxes. ARC-CT combines three components: (1) an Anato- myQFormer localizing evidence via queries constrained by automatically generated organ masks; (2) a label-Jaccard soft InfoNCE objective in- tegrating the standard one-hot target with the label-set overlap of each pair, which reduces false-negative penalties between studies that share clinical findings; and (3) an organ-level alignment loss connecting mask- pooled visual features to organ-specific report text extracted offline with a large language model. ARC-CT achieves a 0.86 mask-free macro AUC across 18 abnormalities using a compact 3D ResNet-18 backbone. Over- all, ARC-CT outperforms both comparable efficient baselines and sev- eral larger transformer models. Our code and weights are available at https://github.com/arc-ct/arc-ct.

</details>


### [69] [LayerRecall: A State-Conditioned Memory Router for Long-Horizon Consistency in Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28460)
*Yixuan Ding,Jiahao Kong,Wei Huang,Ruijie Quan,Yi Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出LayerRecall，一种基于当前条件、逐层选择性记忆路由的机制，用于自回归视频扩散模型中的长视频生成。它通过仅将检索到的历史K/V状态注入骨干网络中对记忆敏感的层，在保持局部连贯性的同时增强远程上下文恢复能力。同时提出Cross-Horizon Prediction Matching（CHPM）训练策略，利用特权长上下文参考监督有界记忆路由器的预测空间，缓解高质量长视频数据稀缺问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归视频扩散模型采用基于近期上下文的缓存机制生成分块视频，虽能保持局部连续性，但当主体、物体、场景或属性重新出现时会丢失历史线索。现有记忆机制虽能暴露非局部历史信息，但单纯访问历史并不保证有效利用。分析发现视频DiT层对当前、近期和远期上下文具有不同偏好，因此长距离记忆需要同时决定检索什么和在哪里使用。

Method: 提出了LayerRecall记忆路由机制：采用当前条件（current-conditioned）的逐层选择性记忆路由器，检索相关的历史K/V状态，并仅将其注入骨干网络中对记忆敏感的特定层，同时在其他层保留局部注意力。为缓解高质量长视频和显式记忆分配标签的稀缺性，提出Cross-Horizon Prediction Matching（CHPM），利用特权长上下文参考在预测空间监督有界记忆路由器的学习。
